| [`a_launch_alongside_another_never_makes_a_place_the_other_watches_as_empty`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs#L6990) | A launch alongside another never makes a place the other watches as empty. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_launch_alongside_another_reports_nothing_it_did_not_find`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/home_protection_alongside.rs#L36) | A launch alongside another reports nothing it did not find. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_way_a_protected_link_leads_is_watched`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs#L8826) | The watch follows the way a protected link leads inside the home: a further link on it that the app replaces, or a missing part of it that the app creates, is moved aside after the start and the link put back, so the protected name leads where it led before; a launch alongside another takes over what that one saw (review B2 S3, R51). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_way_turned_into_a_link_is_undone_and_never_by_deleting`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs#L8882) | A folder on the way to a watched link that the app swaps for a link of its own is moved aside, also when the link it holds has the same text; a launch alongside another takes over every place that one recorded, also those no longer on today's way; and a place only on the way is never deleted, only moved aside or reported (review of PR 128). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_start_file_reached_through_more_links_or_a_missing_place_cannot_be_planted`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/linked_start_place_ways.rs#L50) | A start file reached through more links or a missing place cannot be planted. | 🆕 new |

**Code:** [`sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs), [`sam-runner/src/kernel.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/kernel.rs), [`sam-core/src/util.rs`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs), [`sam-runner/src/home_shared.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/home_shared.rs), [`sam-core/src/places.rs`](../crates/sam-core/src/places.rs), [`sam-core/src/warnings.rs`](../crates/sam-core/src/warnings.rs)

<a id="17"></a>
### 17. The app can't type into your terminal.

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`output_shown_in_a_terminal_is_filtered_without_the_relay`](../crates/sam-runner/src/launch.rs#L2811) | With its input redirected an app has no terminal relay; its output shown in a terminal still loses clipboard codes and title reports, even split over two pieces. | ✅ |
| [`a_pipe_into_a_terminal_program_passes_through_unchanged`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/pipes.rs#L28) | A pipe into a terminal program passes through unchanged: the tool reads the piped input, its output arrives byte for byte, and it has no terminal of its own. | ✅ |
| [`plain_text_survives_unfinished_and_odd_codes`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L663) | The log's plain text survives every unfinished or odd code: one cut off at the end of the data (no read past it), a title ended by ESC \\ with text after it, a backslash inside a title, and an ESC with intermediate bytes at the very end (found by mutation testing: no test reached these edges). | ✅ |
| [`the_terminal_connection_passes_typing_and_filtered_output`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/terminal_relay.rs#L166) | Typing reaches the app; its output reaches the user filtered (colours pass, a clipboard write does not); the window size is copied at the start and follows a resize; the log and the error head get the text without control codes; the user's terminal is raw while the app runs and gets its settings back afterwards. | ✅ |
| [`ctrl_c_reaches_the_app`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/terminal_relay.rs#L234) | Keys reach the app unchanged: Ctrl+C interrupts the app, not SAM. | ✅ |
| [`closing_the_users_terminal_hangs_up_the_app`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/terminal_relay.rs#L252) | When the user's terminal closes, SAM hangs up the app's terminal: the app ends, and the end says so. | ✅ |
| [`the_log_stays_within_its_limit`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/terminal_relay.rs#L269) | The log keeps at most its limit of terminal output and says so. | ✅ |
| [`something_left_on_the_apps_terminal_does_not_keep_sam_waiting`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/terminal_relay.rs#L293) | An app that leaves something running on its terminal (a helper in the background) does not keep SAM waiting once the app itself has ended. | ✅ |
| [`the_output_is_still_logged_when_the_terminal_cannot_show_it`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/terminal_relay.rs#L317) | When the user's terminal can no longer show anything (its output is gone), SAM stops writing there but keeps the log, and the app runs to its end. | ✅ |
| [`leading_zeros_do_not_smuggle_a_code`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L600) | Leading zeros do not smuggle a code past the filter: terminals read "0052" as 52 and "021" as 21, and so does SAM; a number too long for any real code is removed whole (regression: an 11-digit OSC 52 was cut to 5 and passed on). | ✅ |
| [`a_new_session_unless_the_app_is_on_sams_own_terminal`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs#L4035) | The app gets a new terminal session, unless it runs on SAM's terminal relay. | ✅ |
| [`ordinary_output_passes_unchanged`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L540) | Ordinary terminal output, colors and links pass through the relay unchanged. | ✅ |
| [`clipboard_codes_are_removed`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L578) | Escape codes that read or write the clipboard are removed from an app's terminal output. | ✅ |
| [`title_reports_and_tmux_passthrough_are_removed`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L624) | Escape codes that report the window title or pass through tmux are removed; setting the title stays allowed. | ✅ |
| [`an_escape_inside_a_removed_code_starts_a_new_sequence`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L641) | An escape code hidden inside a removed code cannot slip through. | ✅ |
| [`plain_text_for_the_log`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L650) | Logs get plain text without escape codes. | ✅ |
| [`terminal_apps_get_sams_terminal_relay`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L726) | Apps started from a terminal get SAM's terminal relay; with input from a pipe or a file they get none (input and output pass through), and a terminal program's test start gets a terminal of its own. | ✅ |
| [`typing_and_output_pass_and_leftover_replies_are_discarded`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L765) | A second pseudo-terminal stands in for the user's terminal: SAM's side of it is `Outer`, the test plays the terminal program. | ✅ |
| [`the_app_cannot_reach_the_outer_terminal`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L847) | An app on SAM's terminal relay cannot type into the user's real terminal. | ✅ |
| [`every_hostile_appimage_fails_to_escape`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/evil.rs#L159) | Every hostile AppImage, added and started like any other, fails to get out of its sandbox: it cannot read keys, SAM's settings or another app's files; cannot write autostart entries, shell startup files or host temporary files; cannot type into the terminal, reach the network when it has none, see host processes or change its own app folder; cannot reach systemd, the Flatpak spawn portal, the keyring or a launcher service, nor take over a portal's name on the desktop bus; and a symlink or setuid bit in its image gains it nothing. | ✅ |
| [`a_doubled_escape_does_not_smuggle_a_clipboard_code`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L552) | A doubled ESC in front of a clipboard code does not smuggle it past the filter: the terminal reads the second ESC as the start of the code, so the filter must too. | ✅ |
| [`only_tmux_itself_counts_as_tmux_passthrough`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L688) | Only "tmux;" itself is tmux passthrough: a device control string that merely starts with "t" is not named as one (it is removed as unknown; found by mutation testing). | ✅ |
| [`a_slow_terminals_late_replies_do_not_reach_the_shell`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L889) | A slow terminal that answers requests in a trickle after the app has ended: nothing of it reaches the shell (audit L-F10: one fixed wait of 60 ms let later replies through). | ✅ |
| [`limits_hold_at_their_edge`](../crates/sam-core/src/terminal.rs#L1025) | The limits hold exactly: a sequence at the limit is read, one byte more makes it malformed; numbers go up to 65535. | ✅ |
| [`malformed_pieces_are_recognized`](../crates/sam-core/src/terminal.rs#L1054) | Malformed strings and sequences: a parameter after an intermediate; BEL ends only title-type strings; control characters, 8-bit controls and invalid UTF-8 inside a string; an ESC inside a string starts a new code that is judged on its own. | ✅ |
| [`the_allowed_kinds_pass_unchanged`](../crates/sam-core/src/terminal.rs#L973) | Every kind of harmless sequence on the allow-list passes unchanged, also without the relay. | ✅ |
| [`unfinished_pieces_are_named_at_the_end`](../crates/sam-core/src/terminal.rs#L959) | A piece still unfinished when the output ends is removed and named. | ✅ |
| [`a_sandboxed_terminal_app_cannot_reach_past_the_terminal`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/terminal.rs#L92) | A sandboxed terminal app cannot reach past the terminal. | ✅ |
| [`modes_left_on_are_switched_off_at_the_end`](../crates/sam-core/src/terminal.rs#L917) | Modes the app switched on that would stay on for the shell are switched off at the end (audit L-F11: mouse reporting and bracketed paste stayed on); nothing is added for an app that set none. | ✅ |
| [`nothing_past_the_filter_reaches_past_the_terminal`](../crates/sam-core/tests/terminal_fuzz.rs#L440) | Nothing past the filter reaches past the terminal. | ✅ |
| [`only_known_device_strings_pass`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs#L703) | Of the device control strings only the two that ask for the terminal's capabilities and settings pass; tmux passthrough, programmable keys and anything unknown are removed, the text around them stays. | ✅ |
| [`ordinary_output_passes_unchanged_at_every_split`](../crates/sam-core/src/terminal.rs#L843) | Ordinary output passes unchanged, also split at every point. | ✅ |
| [`requests_pass_only_on_the_relay`](../crates/sam-core/src/terminal.rs#L858) | Requests the terminal answers pass only on SAM's relay, where the answer goes to the app; without it the answer would land in the shell. | ✅ |
| [`the_known_ways_past_the_old_filter_are_closed`](../crates/sam-core/src/terminal.rs#L800) | Each way past the old filter that the audit found (L-F1 a–h): none of them brings a clipboard code or a title report to the terminal. | ✅ |
| [`the_log_keeps_only_text_across_reads`](../crates/sam-core/src/terminal.rs#L939) | The log keeps only the text, also when a sequence is split across reads (the old one was cut per read and could keep half a code). | ✅ |
| [`the_model_recognizes_the_known_attacks`](../crates/sam-core/tests/terminal_fuzz.rs#L514) | The model itself finds what it should: each known way past a filter, read without SAM's filter, counts as dangerous (so a passing fuzz run means something). | ✅ |
| [`what_reaches_past_the_terminal_is_removed`](../crates/sam-core/src/terminal.rs#L884) | What reaches past the terminal or changes it for good is removed; unknown codes are removed too (an allow-list). | ✅ |
| [`the_screen_keeps_only_text_and_sams_colours`](../crates/sam-core/src/terminal.rs#L1089) | What SAM prints keeps its own colours and loses everything else an app's text could bring: string sequences (clipboard, title), other control sequences (title reports, hidden text), the 8-bit forms, carriage returns that overwrite a line, and direction overrides. | ✅ |
| [`plain_text_has_no_hidden_characters`](../crates/sam-core/src/terminal.rs#L1115) | The log and the screen drop the same hidden characters as names do. | ✅ |
| [`json_for_the_screen_is_the_same_data`](../crates/sam-core/src/terminal.rs#L1126) | JSON for the terminal is the same data: hidden characters are written as escapes, which every JSON reader turns back into them. | ✅ |
| [`names_lose_direction_overrides_and_invisible_characters`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L1306) | Names that would show other than they are lose the characters that do it (audit I-F12): a direction override (`invoice\u{202E}fdp.exe` shows as `invoiceexe.pdf`), invisible spaces and joiners between letters, tag characters, 8-bit control codes. | ✅ |
| [`what_sam_prints_about_an_app_cannot_reach_past_the_terminal`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/terminal.rs#L297) | What SAM itself prints about an app (G-F5, L-F3, I-F11, I-F12): the name of its file, the failure summary taken from its error output, its log. | ✅ |
| [`every_printed_line_goes_through_the_screen_filter`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/architecture.rs#L268) | Everything SAM prints goes through one place that removes escape codes and hidden characters from it (`sam_core::terminal::for_screen`, audit G-F5): the plain print macros are refused by the code check (clippy.toml), and in SAM's program code only that one file may allow them. | ✅ |
| [`an_old_log_shows_only_its_text`](../crates/sam-runner/src/launch.rs#L2438) | A log written before SAM kept only the text, or edited by hand, still shows only text (audit L-F3): `sam-jail logs` and bug reports read it through `tail`. | ✅ |
| [`a_descriptor_sam_inherited_never_reaches_the_app`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/launch_hygiene.rs#L196) | Review F6 and the audit's open item: a descriptor SAM inherited (a terminal's or launcher's leak; here a pipe at number 7 without its close-on-exec mark) never reaches the app, which could otherwise write to whatever it leads to; nor does any other descriptor above the standard three. | 🆕 new |
| [`without_close_range_each_descriptor_above_the_standard_three_is_marked`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L773) | Review F6 on kernels before 5.11, which have no close_range: the way through `/proc/self/fd` marks an inherited descriptor close-on-exec, keeps the mark on one that has it, and leaves the standard three alone, which a started program needs. | 🆕 new |

**Code:** [`sam-runner/src/pty.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/pty.rs), [`sam-runner/src/kernel.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/kernel.rs), [`sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs), [`sam-core/src/util.rs`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs)

<a id="18"></a>
### 18. Optional: a writable app folder (changes go to a separate layer); where that's impossible, a read-only start with an explanation.

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`the_overlay_check_believes_bubblewraps_answer`](../crates/sam-core/src/host.rs#L253) | The overlay check believes Bubblewrap's answer: a Bubblewrap that fails means no overlay, even though the check's folders could be made (NFS, eCryptfs, another overlay), and one that succeeds means a working overlay. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_overlay_checks_match_the_real_bubblewrap`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/host_probe.rs#L157) | SAM offers the writable app folder exactly where the real Bubblewrap can give it: overlays only from a Bubblewrap that has them (it lists `--overlay-src`), and only on a folder where a real overlay works. | 🆕 new |
| [`writable_appdir_uses_overlay`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs#L4719) | A writable app folder uses an overlay; the unpacked app itself stays unchanged. | ✅ |
| [`an_app_starts_read_only_when_its_writable_app_folder_cannot_work`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/overlay_fallback.rs#L37) | When the folder for the writable app folder's changes cannot hold an overlay, the app still starts, with a read-only app folder and a warning that says why (regression: on Fedora in a container, SAM's data folder was on an overlay, and the app failed with a raw Bubblewrap error). | ✅ |
| [`a_writable_app_folder_is_offered_only_where_it_can_work`](../crates/sam-runner/src/repair.rs#L1842) | The repair steps offer a writable app folder only where an overlay can work: not where SAM's overlay folder cannot hold one (regression: the step was offered by Bubblewrap's version alone, and on such disks the app then started read-only anyway). | ✅ |
| [`the_app_folder_stays_read_only_unless_writable_is_granted`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs#L5283) | The app's own folder stays read-only unless "writable app folder" is granted, even when SAM has an overlay for it prepared. | ✅ |
| [`overlays_count_where_bubblewrap_lists_the_option`](../crates/sam-core/src/host.rs#L275) | Overlays count where Bubblewrap lists `--overlay-src`, whatever its version says: a backport to 0.10 counts, a 0.11 without the option does not, and `--tmp-overlay` or `--ro-overlay` alone are not it. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_option_counts_only_as_a_word_of_its_own`](../crates/sam-core/src/host.rs#L295) | An option counts only as a word of its own: a longer option that starts or ends with it is a look-alike, and a trailing `,` or `=` does not hide it. | 🆕 new |
| [`empty_help_or_an_empty_option_lists_nothing`](../crates/sam-core/src/host.rs#L307) | No help and no option name list nothing. | 🆕 new |
| [`bubblewrap_options_come_from_a_successful_help_on_standard_output`](../crates/sam-core/src/host.rs#L326) | Bubblewrap's options are read from its help on standard output after a successful run: help that fails, help only on standard error and a program that does not exist all count as "no" (stand-ins; the real Bubblewrap is checked in tests/host_probe.rs). | 🆕 new |

**Code:** [`sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs), [`sam-runner/src/launch.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/launch.rs), [`sam-core/src/host.rs`](../crates/sam-core/src/host.rs)

<a id="41"></a>
### 41. A program in an app that builds a sandbox of its own (such as GTK's image loader) gets it from SAM, built outside the app's sandbox (Room service): never with more than the app, never with less than it asked, always with SAM's protection layers, and never outliving the app.

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`room_service_builds_working_rooms_that_never_get_more_than_their_app`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/room_service.rs#L1168) | Room service builds rooms that work, and no room ever gets more than its app: not under "No network" or the full network, not in an app whose Landlock and syscall filter are off, not in a shared home, not through messages written by hand past the stand-in, not by exchanging a folder for a link while rooms are built; and no room outlives its app. | 🆕 new |
| [`every_room_is_a_subset_of_its_app`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/room_service_compare.rs#L171) | Every room is within its app, for generated requests under no network, the full network, and in an app with Landlock and its syscall filter off: no room reads, writes or reaches anything its app cannot, and no room shows a folder of the computer its app is not shown. | 🆕 new |
| [`under_this_computer_only_a_room_reaches_no_more_than_its_app`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/room_service_local.rs#L27) | Under "This computer only", a room that asks for the network gets an empty network of its own: it reaches no port of the computer (not even one listed for its app) and no socket outside the sandbox. | 🆕 new |
| [`glycin_loads_images_in_a_room_never_without_a_sandbox`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/room_service_glycin.rs#L81) | An image loaded through GTK's real image loader inside a SAM sandbox is decoded in a room of its own, never without a sandbox. | 🆕 new |
| [`rooms_never_run_the_launcher_out_of_descriptors`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/room_service_fd_limit.rs#L47) | Rooms never run the launcher out of descriptors. | 🆕 new |
| [`descriptors_for_rooms_stay_within_the_launchers_limit`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3765) | The descriptors held for rooms stay within what the launcher's limit leaves free, and a reservation gives back what did not come. | 🆕 new |
| [`rooms_asked_at_once_wait_for_descriptors_and_are_all_built`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/room_service_fd_wait.rs#L70) | Rooms asked at once wait for descriptors and are all built. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_waiting_for_descriptors_goes_as_soon_as_a_build_gives_them_back`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3840) | A room whose descriptors do not fit while another room is built waits, and goes as soon as that room gives them back (not when its wait runs out); everything is given back after. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_wait_for_descriptors_ends_at_once_when_the_service_stops`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3864) | The stop of the service ends a room's wait for descriptors at once, and a room asking after the stop does not wait at all. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_wait_for_descriptors_gives_up_at_its_deadline`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3886) | A wait for descriptors that never come ends when its time is up, with nothing left counted for it. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_that_cannot_fit_even_alone_is_refused_without_waiting`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3910) | A room that would not fit even with nothing else held (its own descriptors and the connections its request holds are more than the whole budget) is refused at once, without waiting; one that exactly fills the budget is not. | 🆕 new |
| [`rooms_waiting_for_descriptors_go_in_line_while_rooms_are_built`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3927) | Rooms wait in line while rooms are built: a later room waits behind the first even when it would fit now (so a big room is not passed again and again by small ones), and goes as soon as the first leaves the line (here: its wait ran out). | 🆕 new |
| [`while_nothing_is_being_built_a_room_that_fits_may_pass_one_that_waits`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3963) | While nothing is being built, nothing the first in line waits for comes back soon (here the connections of a request hold the budget): a later room that fits goes first instead of waiting behind it, and the first goes once enough is given back. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_later_room_never_passes_the_first_in_line_when_that_one_fits`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4004) | While nothing is being built, a later room passes the first in line only when the first cannot go now: when what was given back lets the first go (here exactly), the later room waits for it, even though it would fit itself, and goes after it. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_waiting_for_descriptors_goes_when_a_request_being_read_gives_back_what_did_not_come`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4052) | A request being read counts room for every connection it may pass before each receive, and gives back at once what did not come: a room that waited only for that room goes as soon as it is given back, not when some room is released later or its wait runs out. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_request_being_read_waits_for_rooms_being_built_and_new_rooms_wait_for_it`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4087) | A request being read when rooms being built hold part of what it may need waits for them to give it back, and gets all it asked for, not only what was left; no new room is built while it waits (one that would fit waits), and that room goes once the request has its share. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_request_being_read_waits_only_while_rooms_are_built_and_only_until_its_end`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4130) | A request being read waits for rooms being built no longer than its reading may take, and not after the service stopped; then it gets what is left. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_device_place_missing_at_the_start_leaves_the_floor_plan`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3451) | A device place missing when the app starts is not in the app, so it leaves the floor plan: a device plugged in later never reaches a room by its name. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_dev_asked_for_without_devices_has_no_working_devices`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2271) | The app's /dev asked for without working devices: the same few nodes, as Bubblewrap shows them without devices, never its /dev with working ones. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_device_place_the_app_has_read_only_stays_read_only`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2841) | The app's own `--remount-ro` over a device place counts in rooms too: never writable there, working devices stay read-only. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_device_place_is_shown_only_as_a_whole`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2768) | A device place is shown only as a whole: a path below it is never looked up by name (a link the app made in /dev/shm could lead to a file the app cannot see). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_failed_start_is_reported_whatever_numbers_the_room_takes`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3522) | A failed start of the room's Bubblewrap is reported as one even when the room's connections take every low number (where the report's own pipe would be): never a room that seems started. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_gets_its_connections_at_their_numbers`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3553) | The room's Bubblewrap gets each connection at its number, its standard streams on /dev/null when not passed, and its exit code. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_record_from_before_room_service_keeps_its_checksum`](../crates/sam-core/src/model.rs#L823) | A record saved before Room service existed (no `room_service` key) reads with Room service on and writes back exactly as it was, so its checksum holds and no app looks changed outside SAM after an update. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_missing_helper_names_only_what_is_lost`](../crates/sam-runner/src/launch.rs#L3163) | Without SAM's helper, each warning names only what is really lost: no word about Landlock and seccomp when the app wanted neither, and Room service named when it is lost. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_helper_comes_for_each_of_its_jobs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/launch.rs#L3144) | SAM's helper goes into the sandbox for each of its jobs on its own (Landlock, the syscall filter, a probe, Room service), so an app with Room service off still gets its layers; a probe has no rooms. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_first_unusable_option_is_found_where_bubblewrap_reads_options`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1709) | The first option a room never gets is found where the stand-in looks for it: up to the end of the options, `--` or the command, never past the end of the arguments. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_answer_to_a_stand_in_that_left_never_ends_the_launcher`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3475) | An answer to a stand-in that has already gone never ends SAM's launcher, also with SIGPIPE at its default (as `sam-jail run` has it). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_receive_keeps_no_more_connections_than_counted`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3504) | A receive keeps at most the connections it has room for: one more is refused, never kept uncounted (CMSG_SPACE's rounding would fit it). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_helper_folder_comes_first_on_path_only_where_it_holds_helpers`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs#L8726) | SAM's helper folder /run/sam/bin comes first on the app's PATH only when there is something in it for the app: the link openers (they need the desktop bus) and Room service's stand-in for Bubblewrap. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_program_needs_a_loader_only_when_a_program_header_asks_for_one`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L2580) | A program asks for a loader when any of its program headers says so, first or later; a program without one (as SAM's static room helper) needs none, also when it is as short as it can be. | 🆕 new |
| [`room_service_runs_only_with_a_static_helper`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L2606) | Room service runs only with a static room helper: none built, or one built to need a loader, even when not empty, turns it off (mutation run 2026-11-24: a dynamic helper was accepted unnoticed). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_room_helper_refuses_a_place_that_is_not_the_one_sam_checked`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L2623) | SAM's room helper ends the room before anything else when a place is not the file, folder or device SAM checked (device and inode): the defence against a Bubblewrap that mounts places by name, which the app could swap while the room is built. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_launcher_keeps_two_descriptors_for_each_connection_for_itself`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L2716) | What the launcher keeps for itself is never given to rooms: 128 of its own, and two for each connection it may have open (the connection itself, and a second one while its room runs). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_sam_ends_is_killed_and_ends_with_the_killed_code`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L2745) | A room SAM ends is killed at once and ends as killed (exit code 137, as a shell shows it); its status stays known, and ending it again changes nothing. | 🆕 new |
| [`waiting_for_a_process_that_is_no_child_fails_at_once`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L2784) | Waiting for a process that is no room of this launcher fails at once (the kernel says it is no child), never waits on forever. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_under_a_low_limit_gets_its_connections_and_keeps_lower_limits`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L2839) | Under a low descriptor limit a room still gets a connection at a number above it (the moves use the hard limit for a moment), then runs with the launcher's own limit; a program's lower limit is kept whether only its soft or only its hard limit is lower, and a higher one is never given. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_start_that_fails_while_moving_connections_is_reported_with_its_cause`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L2871) | A start that fails while the connections are moved is reported with its own cause, whichever move finds no number: never a room that seems started. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_signal_while_a_room_starts_does_not_fail_the_start`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L2916) | A signal that comes while SAM waits to hear whether a room's Bubblewrap started (the launcher gets signals) is no failed start: the room starts, and SAM keeps track of it. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_gets_exactly_its_connections_and_none_overwrites_another`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L2970) | A room's program has exactly its connections, each at its number, and /dev/null where it passed no standard stream: none is lost or overwritten by another on the way, and nothing else of the launcher stays open, also what would not close by itself when a program starts. | 🆕 new |
| [`without_close_range_a_room_still_gets_exactly_its_connections`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L2979) | The same on a kernel before 5.11, which has no close_range: each descriptor of the launcher is then marked on its own, and none reaches the room. | 🆕 new |
| [`without_close_range_and_proc_a_room_still_gets_exactly_its_connections`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L2990) | The same without close_range and without /proc: each number up to the launcher's soft limit is then marked. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_never_gets_a_variable_without_a_valid_name`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3080) | The room's rules never carry an environment variable without a valid name: none without a name, none whose name holds '=' (SAM's helper would read it as another variable). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_place_is_opened_beneath_its_source_as_a_handle_through_no_link`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3115) | A place beneath a source is opened as a handle only (never read, so also a file nobody may read), closed when a program starts, and only strictly beneath its source through no link: a link, a way out, or a missing path is an error with its own cause. | 🆕 new |
| [`sams_own_descriptors_work_at_any_number_and_fail_when_none_is_left`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3166) | SAM's own descriptors (a place's handle, a room's data) work at any number the kernel gives, also 0 when the launcher was started without standard input; with no number left they are an error, never a handle that is not there. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_place_on_a_read_only_file_system_is_known_as_one`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3217) | A place on a read-only file system is known as one (a room never gets it writable), and one on a writable file system is not. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_request_is_recorded_as_asked_by_the_program_that_started_the_requester`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3252) | The start record names who asked: the program that started the requesting one (here this test program, which starts a copy of itself that connects). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_is_named_by_its_programs_file_name`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3295) | A room is named in the start record by its program's file name; "?" when the request names no program. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_receive_takes_connections_close_on_exec_and_never_waits`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3313) | A received connection closes when a program starts (it reaches a room only at the number the service gives it); a receive with nothing waiting returns at once, and the end of the stream reads as no bytes, not as an error. | 🆕 new |
| [`credentials_that_come_with_a_request_are_never_taken_for_connections`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3340) | Only passed connections are taken from what comes with a request: the sender's credentials, which the kernel adds where the socket asks for them, are never taken for connections. | 🆕 new |
| [`waiting_for_a_request_ends_in_time_and_when_the_start_ends`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3367) | Waiting for a request ends with an error when nothing comes in time or when the app's start ends, and returns once something comes. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_signal_during_the_wait_for_a_request_does_not_end_it`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3386) | A signal that interrupts the wait for a request (the launcher gets signals, such as one when a child ends) does not end the wait: the request is still read. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_request_never_brings_more_connections_than_allowed_in_parts`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3423) | A request never brings more connections than a request may have, also when they come in parts: once the most have come, a later part with one more is refused. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_urgent_byte_before_the_request_does_not_end_its_reading`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5507) | A stray byte the program marks urgent (out of band) makes the connection look readable while there is nothing to read yet: the service waits on for the request and reads it, never takes that for a refusal. | 🆕 new |
| [`too_many_connections_are_refused_with_the_right_reason`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5534) | Too many connections are refused for what they are: more than one request may pass ("passes too many"), or more than the launcher has room for now ("held for rooms at once", which says the app is not at fault). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_request_of_the_largest_size_is_read_and_one_byte_more_is_refused`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5568) | A request of exactly the largest size is read whole; one byte more is refused before it is read. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_empty_send_succeeds_and_a_send_to_a_closed_connection_fails`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5602) | Sending nothing is done at once, and a send to a connection whose other end is gone says so (it never reads as sent). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_send_taken_in_part_still_sends_everything`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5617) | A send the kernel takes only in part (here a signal ends the wait for room in a full connection) still sends the rest: the other side gets every byte and the connection that came with the first part, and the send returns once all is sent. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_standin_dies_with_its_parent_only_when_asked`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5683) | The stand-in ends with the program that started it exactly when Bubblewrap is asked to (`--die-with-parent`), not otherwise: it sets that before it even looks for Room service (here there is none, so it then ends with an error). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_quit_asked_before_the_request_went_out_reaches_the_room_once`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5444) | A SIGTERM to the stand-in before its request went out (a Close or a logout while a program starts its room) reaches the room once the request is sent, so the room's program gets its time to save. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_quit_asked_as_the_request_went_out_reaches_the_room_once`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5454) | A SIGTERM just as the request went out reaches the room once, not twice (a second SIGTERM is "quit now" to some programs). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_helper_is_the_stand_in_only_under_bubblewraps_name`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5717) | SAM's helper is the stand-in exactly when it is started under Bubblewrap's name: by the path the program started (whatever name it gave) or by the name it gave (whatever path), never under its own name or a name that only contains Bubblewrap's. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_version_line_keeps_only_plain_characters_and_at_most_60`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5742) | The Bubblewrap version line the stand-in answers keeps only plain characters (letters, digits, space, `.`, `_`, `-`), at most 60, and no space or line end around it. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_start_record_keeps_its_room_lines_and_counts_the_rest`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4440) | The start record keeps at most MAX_ROOM_RECORDS lines about rooms and MAX_RECORDS refusals, in that order, and counts the rest: one line more than fits is counted, never kept. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_stop_with_nothing_left_is_quick_and_adds_nothing`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4461) | A stop with nothing left to wait for returns at once and adds no line of its own to the start record (no "0 more …", no "0 requests still unfinished"). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_stop_names_requests_still_unfinished`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4474) | A request still not finished when the service stops is named in the start record after the short wait, never lost. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_socket_path_of_the_longest_length_still_opens`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4490) | The service's socket may have a path of 107 bytes, the most a Unix socket's address holds (108 with its closing NUL byte); one byte more is refused as too long. | 🆕 new |
| [`dropping_the_service_ends_it_like_a_stop`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4767) | A service that is dropped without a stop ends like one: it stops listening and its socket is gone. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_fault_in_a_request_gives_back_its_slot_and_connection`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4520) | A fault (a panic) in a request never keeps its connection or its room slot counted: after more faults than rooms may run at once, every count is back to nothing, a later request is served, and the stop reports no request as unfinished. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_fault_after_a_room_started_ends_that_room_and_answers`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4706) | A fault (a panic) after a room's Bubblewrap has started never leaves that room behind: the room is ended and waited for (no child of the launcher is left unwaited) and leaves the service's list, the stand-in hears why at once (instead of waiting until the app's start ends), the start record names it, and a later request is served. | 🆕 new |
| [`rooms_need_both_options_that_show_places_by_handle`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4784) | A Bubblewrap that knows only one of the two options that show a place by handle builds no room either. | 🆕 new |
| [`rooms_and_raw_arguments_count_option_values_alike`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1605) | A room's arguments and the person's raw arguments are read with the same number of values for every Bubblewrap option (independent review of PR 133: two tables could drift apart). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_descriptor_limit_leaving_rooms_fewer_than_64_is_recorded`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4811) | The start record says so when the launcher's descriptor limit leaves rooms fewer than 64 descriptors (so most rooms are refused), and only then: at 64 it says nothing, at 63 it does. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_long_answer_arrives_whole_through_partial_and_interrupted_sends`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4852) | An answer reaches the stand-in whole even when the send stops part way (a signal while the connection is full) and when a signal interrupts it before anything went: never cut short, never more, never a part twice. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_answer_to_a_stand_in_that_left_returns_at_once`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4905) | An answer to a stand-in that has already gone gives up at once: the service never keeps trying. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_of_exactly_the_most_places_is_not_refused_for_their_number`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4924) | A room may have exactly MAX_PLACES places: such a room passes the place limit (here it is then refused for the descriptors, as none are left for rooms), and only one more place is refused for their number. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_leaves_out_only_missing_paths_asked_with_try_and_never_follows_a_link`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4972) | A path the request names below a place and asks for with `-try` is left out of the room when it is missing; missing without `-try`, the room is refused, never built without it. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_place_is_writable_in_a_room_only_where_the_app_can_write`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5013) | A place asked for writable is writable in the room only where the app can write (its Landlock rules); elsewhere it is read-only and the start record says so. | 🆕 new |
| [`rooms_one_after_another_never_run_out_of_slots`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5053) | Rooms one after another each give their slot back: more rooms than may run at once are all built, one by one. | 🆕 new |
| [`every_room_ends_with_the_start_whatever_other_rooms_did_before`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5308) | Every room still running ends with the app's start, also after other rooms ended before it: one whose layers were never confirmed, one whose program ended. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_signal_to_the_rooms_watcher_never_ends_the_room`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5342) | A signal that interrupts the room's watcher (the launcher handles terminal resizes, say) is not its stand-in leaving: the room runs on to its own end. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_source_on_a_removable_drive_is_remembered_not_held`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3631) | A source on a drive that may be unmounted is not held open (the drive stays free to unmount while the app runs); a room still shows it, but never another folder put in its place since the start. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_link_planted_above_a_remembered_place_is_never_followed`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3668) | A place on a drive that may be unmounted is opened again for a room where it really was at the start, following no link. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_device_place_is_shown_only_while_it_is_the_one_the_app_was_given`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5177) | A device place of the app is shown by its name (Bubblewrap cannot show a device by handle), so a room shows it only while it is still what the app was given when it started, and SAM's helper is told what it must be in the room. | 🆕 new |
| [`places_a_later_mount_of_the_room_covers_are_not_checked_in_the_room`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5240) | A place of the app that a later mount of the room covers (a room's own `--dev /dev` over the app's `/dev/dri`, as in the common `--ro-bind / / --dev /dev`, or a `--tmpfs` over a folder of the app) is not shown in the room, so SAM's helper is not told to check it, and the room is built as asked. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_device_place_that_cannot_be_checked_at_the_start_refuses_every_room`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5289) | A device place of the app that exists but cannot be checked when the app starts (here a link to itself) refuses every room of that start, as other places do: it is never left out quietly. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_place_that_cannot_be_opened_at_the_start_refuses_every_room`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5116) | A place of the app that exists but cannot be opened when the app starts (here a link to itself; in practice no descriptors left) never leaves the floor plan quietly: it may be the read-only file that protects something inside a writable folder, which every room would then get writable while the app has it read-only. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_remount_over_a_missing_try_place_keeps_the_place_below_read_only`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L5075) | A remount over a `-try` place whose source is missing: Bubblewrap skips that place and makes the one below it read-only for the app; after the start's floor plan leaves the missing one out, the place below is read-only for rooms too (review of PR 124). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_place_below_a_writable_folder_needs_its_protections_pinned`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2642) | A place below a folder the app can write is shown only when the app cannot move its protected or hidden places there: every folder between that folder and such a place is pinned (a mount point of its own). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_hidden_file_below_a_writable_folder_needs_its_folders_pinned`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2691) | SAM hides a credential file reached through a link (`~/.netrc` into `~/dotfiles`) with an empty file over it: that counts as a hiding as an empty folder does. | 🆕 new |
| [`nothing_of_the_room_lies_inside_a_device_place`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2585) | A device place the app has (perhaps a folder it or the person can write) is shown only as a whole too: nothing of the room's own lies inside it in any order, and an own place inside it gets no Landlock write right. | 🆕 new |
| [`read_only_places_inside_a_writable_request_are_recorded`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2625) | Places inside a request's place that the app has read-only are recorded as capped when the request asked for them writable. | 🆕 new |
| [`rooms_keep_their_first_process_and_their_order`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2556) | --as-pid-1 is not passed into a room (Bubblewrap's own first process stays, and the room ends with SAM), and says so; a request's --remount-ro comes after its own links, as Bubblewrap would apply them; a link to a folder holding SAM's place is refused. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_left_out_setting_shows_its_default`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/perms.rs#L1349) | A switch whose key the saved form leaves out while it is at its default shows that default: Room service shows on for an app that has it on (every app, unless turned off), off once turned off. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_link_named_first_is_never_made_inside_a_later_place`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2489) | The room's own link is never made inside a place shown from the app, also when it was named before that place (links are made last, after every mount, so a link in a read-only place of the app would lead it onto the computer). | 🆕 new |
| [`read_only_asks_and_unwritable_own_places_are_kept_and_said`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2512) | A place asked for read-only keeps what the room makes for it (the app's hiding places, its /dev) read-only; own new places that cannot be written are recorded. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_apps_remount_is_not_recursive`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2329) | The app's own --remount-ro makes read-only only the mount at its place, as Bubblewrap's (not recursive): a place mounted below it before keeps its rights, in the app as in its rooms. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_remount_reaches_the_mount_bubblewrap_finds_on_top`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2355) | A remount reaches whatever mount Bubblewrap finds on top at its place: also one at the root (the person's own `--bind / /` before SAM's final `--remount-ro /`), and the mount below a `-try` place, which Bubblewrap skips when its source is missing and Room service then leaves out. | 🆕 new |
| [`drives_that_may_be_unmounted_are_known_by_their_file_system`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3575) | A drive is one a person may unmount when it is not the file system of the root or the home, or lies under /media, /mnt, /run/media. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_drive_is_known_by_its_mount_or_its_file_systems_id`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3718) | A drive is known by its mount's unique ID where the kernel gives one; before Linux 6.8 by the file-system ID listed for its block device; otherwise by nothing more than device and inode. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_drive_mounted_anew_at_its_place_is_not_the_one_the_app_was_shown`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L4644) | A drive unmounted and another mounted at its place, with the same device and the same inode (as any two FAT sticks' top folders at the same port), is not the one the app was shown: a room is refused it. | 🆕 new |
| [`rooms_need_a_bubblewrap_that_shows_places_by_handle`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_service.rs#L3596) | Room service starts only with a Bubblewrap that can show places by handle: with an older one, programs keep the computer's own Bubblewrap instead of a stand-in that could build no room. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_single_device_file_is_shown_by_its_exact_name`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2817) | A device that is a single file, asked for at its own place, is shown by exactly its name (no "/" after it, which a file cannot have). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_shows_only_what_sam_opened_whatever_bubblewrap_does`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/room_service_identity.rs#L238) | A room shows only what sam opened whatever bubblewrap does. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_place_whose_mount_was_moved_away_and_the_original_put_back_is_refused`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/room_service_identity.rs#L320) | After Bubblewrap mounted a room's place, a program outside the app's sandbox that can write the folder holding it (another program of the person's) moves the place away (the room's mount goes with its name) and puts the very same file back at the name (a second name made beforehand, a hard link). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_same_object_at_the_root_of_a_mount_passes`](../crates/sam-core/src/place_check.rs#L303) | The root of the file system is a mount in every mount namespace: the object SAM chose, as the root of a mount, passes. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_link_at_the_place_is_refused`](../crates/sam-core/src/place_check.rs#L312) | A link at the place, or on its way, is refused, even when it leads to exactly the object SAM chose. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_different_object_is_refused`](../crates/sam-core/src/place_check.rs#L342) | Another file, folder or device than SAM chose is refused, whatever differs (the inode, or the device). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_place_that_is_not_the_root_of_a_mount_is_refused_where_a_mount_is_expected`](../crates/sam-core/src/place_check.rs#L361) | The very object SAM chose is refused where a mount was made but the place is not the root of one (a folder or a file inside a mount), and so is any place when the kernel does not say: the check fails closed, with a message that names the cause. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_mount_root_answer_reads_only_the_mount_root_bit`](../crates/sam-core/src/place_check.rs#L391) | The kernel's answer on the mount root: without the attribute's bit in the mask there is no answer (the check fails closed, as `MountUnknown`); with it, the attribute decides. | 🆕 new |
| [`only_a_link_is_reported_as_a_link`](../crates/sam-core/src/place_check.rs#L430) | Only a link on the way is reported as a link: a place that is missing or may not be opened says it cannot be opened and names the error. | 🆕 new |
| [`only_absolute_paths_are_opened_without_links`](../crates/sam-core/src/place_check.rs#L446) | A path that is not absolute is never opened without links (it would depend on the current folder). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_statx_struct_is_the_kernels`](../crates/sam-core/src/place_check.rs#L275) | The kernel's struct, as the C library describes it: the fields read here lie where the kernel writes them, and nothing is written past the end. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_left_out_setting_can_be_turned_back_on`](../crates/sam-profile/src/lib.rs#L796) | A setting the saved form leaves out while at its default (Room service is written only when off) can be changed back: turned off, then on again from an old copy, it is left out again, never null. | 🆕 new |
| [`changed_room_requests_never_crash_the_readers`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/fuzz_targets.rs#L438) | Room service's readers: changed messages and changed Bubblewrap argument lists never crash the service's reader, the stand-in's reader or the rules, and whatever the rules accept stays within them (every place a plain absolute path, shown at the same place, from beneath a folder of the app's floor plan). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_room_dies_with_sams_launcher`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/room_teardown.rs#L52) | A room of an app (built by Room service, outside the app's sandbox) ends when SAM's launcher is killed outright. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_place_the_app_has_hidden_by_a_later_mount_stays_hidden`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1927) | A grant inside SAM's folder that a later tmpfs hides in the app (audit S-F13) stays hidden: the last mount over a place counts, not the deepest. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_apps_own_restrictions_in_its_arguments_count`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2065) | The apps own restrictions in its arguments count. | 🆕 new |
| [`new_things_never_inside_a_place_shown_from_the_app`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2161) | Bubblewrap makes folders and links on the computer when it is told to make them inside a shown folder, following any link the app left there: the room's own new things only in its own space. | 🆕 new |
| [`sams_own_place_in_a_room_is_out_of_reach`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2238) | SAM's own place in a room (its helper and the room's rules): no request may name it, cover it with a link, or make things in it. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_own_place_above_the_apps_places_gives_no_write_right`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2221) | An own place gives no Landlock write right over places of the app below it. | 🆕 new |
| [`bwrap_args_are_read_as_bubblewrap_reads_them`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1615) | Bwrap args are read as bubblewrap reads them. | 🆕 new |
| [`options_a_room_never_gets_are_refused_by_name`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1644) | Options a room never gets are refused by name. | 🆕 new |
| [`missing_values_and_commands_are_refused`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1730) | Missing values and commands are refused. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_request_message_is_read_strictly`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1737) | A request message is read strictly. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_wrong_connection_number_is_named_in_the_refusal`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1774) | A wrong connection number is refused with a reason that names it: one at or above the limit (with the limit and what the program can do), a negative one, one given twice. | 🆕 new |
| [`paths_must_be_plain_and_absolute`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1808) | Paths must be plain and absolute. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_place_is_shown_only_at_the_same_place`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1825) | A place is shown only at the same place. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_place_comes_from_its_floor_plan_entry_not_a_name`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1830) | A place comes from its floor plan entry not a name. | 🆕 new |
| [`asking_for_a_parent_shows_the_apps_places_inside_it`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1849) | `/usr` is not one place of the app but several: the room gets each, and SAM's links, in the floor plan's order. | 🆕 new |
| [`writable_only_where_the_app_writes`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1878) | Writable only where the app writes. | 🆕 new |
| [`nothing_is_placed_inside_a_place_the_app_can_write`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1897) | A place the app can write is shown only as a whole: the home, with its protected and hidden places below it, is refused (Bubblewrap would place them by names the app can change); a folder inside it with nothing below is fine; the hidden place itself shows nothing. | 🆕 new |
| [`everything_asked_is_the_apps_view_without_its_private_places`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1950) | Everything asked is the apps view without its private places. | 🆕 new |
| [`what_the_app_cannot_see_is_refused_or_skipped`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1986) | What the app cannot see is refused or skipped. | 🆕 new |
| [`devices_only_the_apps_and_only_as_asked`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L1998) | Devices only the app's, and only working where the request asked for working devices. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_network_only_with_the_apps_full_network_and_never_when_given_up`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2040) | The network only with the apps full network and never when given up. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_environment_is_kept_in_order_for_the_program`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2104) | The environment is kept in order for the program. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_programs_own_filter_must_be_a_passed_connection`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2124) | The programs own filter must be a passed connection. | 🆕 new |
| [`permissions_only_on_the_rooms_own_new_places`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2135) | Permissions only on the rooms own new places. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_rooms_folder_is_asked_or_safe`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2892) | The rooms folder is asked or safe. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_floor_plan_with_an_unknown_option_gives_no_rooms`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2907) | A floor plan with an unknown option gives no rooms. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_request_is_read_up_to_each_limit_and_refused_one_beyond_it`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2922) | Every limit of a request holds at exactly its size: a request right at a limit is read, one a step over it is refused for that reason. | 🆕 new |
| [`modes_sizes_and_host_names_of_the_rooms_own_places_are_checked_each_in_its_form`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L2998) | The room's own new places take a mode (--perms, for the next --tmpfs or --dir) and a size (--size, for the next --tmpfs only) as Bubblewrap does, each checked in its own form; --chmod takes a mode up to 7777; a host name is passed on as asked. | 🆕 new |
| [`sams_own_arguments_are_read_into_the_floor_plan_as_bubblewrap_reads_them`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L3045) | SAM's own arguments are read as Bubblewrap reads them: the app folder laid as an overlay is shown to rooms from its unchanged source, read-only; the last of the network options counts; an option a room never gets leaves the app without rooms. | 🆕 new |
| [`sams_own_place_is_out_of_reach_through_the_apps_places_too`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L3090) | SAM's own place stays out of reach also where a request reaches it only through the app's places (an app place over the root, or one inside SAM's place), and a refusal for it names it before anything else wrong in the request. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_mode_change_named_first_never_lies_inside_a_place_the_app_can_write`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L3135) | A mode change the room asks for never lies inside a place the app can write, also when it was named before that place. | 🆕 new |
| [`only_places_given_with_fewer_rights_than_asked_are_recorded`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L3157) | Only a place asked for writable and given read-only is recorded as given with fewer rights: one asked for read-only, or given writable as asked, is not. | 🆕 new |
| [`nothing_of_the_room_lies_inside_a_proc_of_its_own`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L3396) | A /proc of the room's own is mounted while Bubblewrap builds the room, and its links (/proc/self/root, /proc/N/root, /proc/self/fd/N) lead out of the room: to the build stage, where the computer's root is at /oldroot, and to the folders SAM hands Bubblewrap. | 🆕 new |
| [`nothing_of_the_room_lies_at_or_behind_a_link_copied_from_the_app`](../crates/sam-runner/src/room_rules.rs#L3192) | The app's links that a room has too (copied with the app's view of a place that holds them: SAM's `/usr/lib64` link here, the links of the app's /dev) count like the room's own: nothing is made, changed or shown at or behind one, in whatever order it was named, since Bubblewrap would follow the link wherever it leads. | 🆕 new |

## D. Starting and running

<a id="19"></a>
### 19. Starting from SAM's window, from the menu, or from the terminal always goes through the sandbox.

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`sams_menu_entry_starts_the_app_only_through_sam`](../crates/sam-intake/src/desktop.rs#L424) | SAM's menu entry starts the app through sam-jail-run, never the AppImage directly. | ✅ |
| [`app_metadata_cannot_add_lines_to_sams_menu_entry`](../crates/sam-intake/src/desktop.rs#L326) | SAM's menu entry starts the app only through SAM's sandbox: no value taken from the app (name, comment, categories, file types, window class) can add a line such as a second "Exec=" to it, even from a record changed outside SAM. | ✅ |
| [`a_hostile_name_adds_no_line_to_sams_menu_entry`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/adversarial.rs#L169) | B27: a hostile AppImage cannot add lines to the menu entry SAM writes for it. | ✅ |
| [`menu_entry_paths_are_quoted_against_spaces_and_dollar_signs`](../crates/sam-intake/src/desktop.rs#L361) | Program paths in menu entries are quoted so spaces and dollar signs cannot change the command. | ✅ |
| [`launcher_mime_types_drop_hijack_targets`](../crates/sam-intake/src/desktop.rs#L472) | Launcher mime types drop hijack targets. | ✅ |
| [`launcher_drops_hijack_mime_types`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/adversarial.rs#L114) | M1: a launcher never registers URL schemes, directories or executables. | ✅ |
| [`hide_and_restore_user_entry`](../crates/sam-intake/src/menu.rs#L413) | An unsandboxed menu entry in the user's folder is hidden on request and restored exactly as it was. | ✅ |
| [`system_entry_gets_override`](../crates/sam-intake/src/menu.rs#L614) | An unsandboxed menu entry in a system folder is hidden through an entry in the user's folder, without touching the system file. | ✅ |
| [`found_appimages_get_a_menu_entry_only_with_a_sandbox`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/overview.rs#L13) | AppImages are listed before they have a sandbox; only creating the sandbox adds SAM's menu entry and hides unsandboxed ones, and removing it puts them back. | ✅ |
| [`shell_quoting_keeps_spaces_and_quotes_intact`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L1068) | Shell quoting keeps spaces, quotes and empty strings intact. | ✅ |
| [`the_window_id_matches_the_name_of_sams_menu_entry`](../crates/sam-core/src/paths.rs#L475) ⟲ | Regression: sandboxed windows carried the app's id as their app ID while SAM's menu entry is "sam-<id>.desktop", so the taskbar found no entry and showed a generic icon. | ✅ |
| [`sam_never_runs_a_helper_the_user_can_change`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/trusted_helpers.rs#L61) | Sam never runs a helper the user can change. | ✅ |
| [`only_programs_the_user_cannot_change_may_run`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L1210) | SAM runs only programs the user cannot change: one in a folder the user owns is refused, also behind a link, and a link there that leads to a system program is followed to it (audit L-F12). | ✅ |
| [`as_root_only_what_no_one_else_can_change_may_run`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L1133) | Run as root, SAM takes a program only where root alone can change it: owned by root, not writable for others, and writable for a group only when that group is root's. | ✅ |
| [`menu_entries_name_sams_launcher_by_its_whole_path`](../crates/sam-intake/src/desktop.rs#L375) | A menu entry names SAM's launcher by its whole path, so the desktop never looks it up on a `$PATH` an app may have put a copy into (audit G-F10); an older entry that names it alone is reported, and the window rewrites it at its next start. | ✅ |
| [`an_installed_window_runs_only_the_tool_next_to_it`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/backend.rs#L388) | An installed (release) window runs only the command line tool next to it, never one in a neighbouring `debug` or `release` folder that an app with write access to the home could create (audit G-F10). | ✅ |
| [`an_installed_window_ignores_a_program_named_in_its_environment`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/backend.rs#L413) | An installed (release) window never runs a program that a variable in its environment names (`SAM_CLI`, `SAM_RUN`); only a developer build does (owner decision 16 of 2026-11-23). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_release_build_ignores_developer_variables`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L864) | A release build ignores a developer variable that is set; only a developer build takes it, and an empty value means "not set" (owner decision 16 of 2026-11-23). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_release_build_ignores_sams_folder_switches`](../crates/sam-core/src/paths.rs#L289) | A release build ignores SAM's folder switches (`SAM_CONFIG_DIR`, `SAM_DATA_DIR`, `SAM_APPLICATIONS_DIR`, `SAM_RUNTIME_DIR`): it keeps its settings, data and menu entries where it keeps them otherwise and its runtime folders in the session's runtime folder, so a variable in the desktop's environment never moves an installed SAM to other settings and app records (owner decision of 2026-11-27). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_developer_build_follows_sams_folder_switches`](../crates/sam-core/src/paths.rs#L302) | A developer build (tests, work on SAM itself) follows SAM's four folder switches. | 🆕 new |
| [`only_a_developer_build_counts_its_folders_as_moved_by_a_switch`](../crates/sam-core/src/paths.rs#L372) | SAM's window runs as a window of its own and leaves the desktop's entry for SAM alone only when a developer switch really moves its settings or data: in a developer build. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_release_build_trusts_no_folder_of_test_tools`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L1261) | A release build never trusts the folder of stand-in tools that tests name in `SAM_TRUST_TOOLS_IN`: a tool there that anyone may change is refused, as everywhere else. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_file_name_that_is_not_text_is_refused_by_name`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/src/bin/sam-jail-run.rs#L580) | A file name with bytes that are not text is named in the message, never passed on changed and never a crash (audit S-F15). | ✅ |
| [`a_folder_name_that_is_not_text_stops_the_start`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs#L4386) | A folder whose name is not valid text (Linux allows any bytes) is never passed on with a replaced character, which would name another folder (audit S-F15): the start is refused with the folder named. | ✅ |
| [`a_protected_place_whose_name_is_not_text_stops_the_start`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs#L4410) | A place SAM hides or locks whose name is not valid text stops the start: SAM's own folders, key folders, start files and SAM's own programs, also the start folder and a writable app folder. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_appimage_never_covers_sams_own_files`](../crates/sam-core/src/sandbox.rs#L429) | An AppImage file named like one of SAM's own files in the sandbox gets a folder of its own instead of covering SAM's (audit S-F17). | ✅ |
| [`an_appimage_called_bin_starts_and_finds_itself`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/odd_names.rs#L84) | An AppImage file called like one of SAM's own files in the sandbox (`bin`) starts, and finds itself at `$APPIMAGE`. | ✅ |
| [`a_percent_sign_in_sams_path_stays_a_percent_sign`](../crates/sam-intake/src/desktop.rs#L411) | A `%` in SAM's own path is written `%%` in the menu entry, where a single one would start a field code like `%F` (audit G-F17). | ✅ |
| [`blocked_file_types_hold_in_any_case_and_old_names`](../crates/sam-intake/src/desktop.rs#L453) | SAM's menu entry never claims links, folders or desktop files, so a sandboxed app cannot take over opening them. | ✅ |
| [`an_app_can_never_replace_the_programs_that_menu_entries_run`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/own_programs.rs#L53) | An app can never replace the programs that menu entries run. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_shown_command_is_the_one_that_runs`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/src/output.rs#L548) | `sam-jail command` shows the sandbox command one option with its values per line, every token in order, and after the real `--` the program with its arguments; joined again it is exactly the command that runs. | 🆕 new |
| [`command_shows_exactly_the_command_that_runs`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/human_views.rs#L263) | `command` shows exactly the command that runs: joined again, its lines are the argument list of `--json command`, the program's own arguments included. | 🆕 new |
| [`every_option_keeps_its_values_on_its_line`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/src/output.rs#L602) | Every option of Bubblewrap stays on one line with its values, also those SAM's own command has that were split across lines before (`--uid 1000`, `--overlay-src`, `--overlay` with three values, `--remount-ro /`, `--json-status-fd 3`). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_user_may_run_only_what_they_neither_own_nor_can_write`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L1150) | A normal user may run only what they neither own nor can write: write access through root's group or an ACL is still write access (the system's answer counts, not the owner), and a program owned by another user that the user cannot write may run. | 🆕 new |
| [`only_the_appimage_this_program_runs_from_is_sams_own`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L1080) | SAM's own AppImage is the one whose mounted folder holds this program, and only while the file exists: values inherited from another AppImage (a terminal shipped as one), or a stale `APPDIR` with its file gone, never make menu entries run another program. | 🆕 new |
| [`only_executable_files_count_as_programs`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L1179) | Only regular files with an execute bit count as programs: not a file without one (a tool an admin switched off with `chmod -x`), not a folder, not a missing path. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_menu_command_names_every_entry_that_starts_the_app_outside`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/commands.rs#L438) | `menu list` on the terminal names an unsandboxed menu entry that starts the app, and after `menu hide` the hidden one; it says that no other entry starts the app only when there is none. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_command_preview_ends_with_the_arguments_given`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/commands.rs#L641) | `command APP -- ARGS` shows the command that `run` would start, with the arguments given at its end. | 🆕 new |
| [`run_hands_the_app_the_arguments_given`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/starts.rs#L217) | `run APP -- ARGS` starts the app with those arguments. | 🆕 new |
| [`desktop_installs_removes_and_refreshes_launchers`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/commands.rs#L695) | `desktop APP install` and `remove` put the app's launcher in the menu and take it out; `desktop all refresh` renews every launcher; other actions are refused. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_app_gets_its_arguments_without_the_separator`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/src/bin/sam-jail-run.rs#L603) | The files from the menu and the arguments typed after the id reach the app as given; only one leading `--` is a separator (the app never sees it as its first argument), a later one is the app's. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_files_from_the_menu_reach_the_app`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/menu_notice.rs#L262) | The files from the menu reach the app, without the `--` that may separate them from the id. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_refused_menu_start_is_told_in_a_notice`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/menu_notice.rs#L161) | A start SAM refuses before any sandbox is built (an app it does not know, a file name that is not text) is told in a notice, naming what went wrong; the start still ends with an error. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_menu_start_the_sandbox_check_refuses_points_to_test`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/menu_notice.rs#L225) | A start the sandbox check refuses (a setting that does not apply) gets the notice that says where to look: the app's Test. | 🆕 new |
| [`only_a_failed_terminal_app_keeps_its_window_open`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/src/bin/sam-jail-run.rs#L632) | A terminal app's window waits for Enter only after a failed end, and only with someone at the terminal; a clean end closes it at once, and a graphical app never waits. | 🆕 new |
| [`values_that_read_like_the_separator_break_no_start`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/launch_hygiene.rs#L319) | Review F5: an option value that reads `--` (an AppImage file called `--`, a terminal type set to it) is never taken for Bubblewrap's separator when SAM adds its helper's status channel: the app starts. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_status_channel_goes_before_bubblewraps_own_separator`](../crates/sam-runner/src/launch.rs#L2623) | Review F5: the helper's status channel goes right before Bubblewrap's own separator, never into an option whose value reads `--` (an AppImage file called `--`, a variable set to it), and the helper's own `--` after the separator stays where it is. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_hidden_entry_no_longer_opens_files`](../crates/sam-intake/src/menu.rs#L483) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, F4): hiding an unsandboxed entry set only `NoDisplay=true`, which takes it out of the menu but leaves it a handler for its file types, so opening a file still started the AppImage without its sandbox. | 🆕 new |
| [`entries_that_still_open_files_are_not_taken_for_hidden`](../crates/sam-intake/src/menu.rs#L546) | An entry out of the menu that still opens files is a way to start the AppImage without its sandbox, so it is offered for hiding; one deleted (`Hidden=true`) or opening nothing counts as hidden elsewhere. | 🆕 new |
