## F. Keeping things safe over time

<a id="27"></a>
### 27. SAM notices app settings that something else changed and asks before starting the app.

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`records_changed_outside_sam_are_recognized`](../crates/sam-storage/src/lib.rs#L864) | A record SAM saved loads as unchanged; a record changed afterwards by something else is recognized as changed outside SAM, and stays marked through SAM's own saves until it is confirmed; a record without SAM's checksum (removed, or from before the checksum) is marked too. | ✅ |
| [`a_save_of_an_older_copy_keeps_the_marks_set_meanwhile`](../crates/sam-storage/src/lib.rs#L951) | A copy of a record read before one of its "look at it first" marks was set, saved later, keeps the mark: a refresh or a profile change that overlapped an "unpack again" no longer undoes it (review L3). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_save_over_an_unreadable_record_drops_nothing_silently`](../crates/sam-storage/src/lib.rs#L1006) | A save over a record SAM cannot read drops nothing silently: a newer SAM's record stays unchanged and the save fails, and over a damaged record or one with another id the copy is saved marked as changed outside SAM (review of PR 120). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_review_fingerprint_covers_what_a_review_confirms`](../crates/sam-storage/src/lib.rs#L1049) | The review fingerprint changes with every notice a review clears or that asks for a look, with the settings and with the AppImage, and stays the same through saves that change none of these (review L4). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_review_from_an_out_of_date_page_confirms_nothing`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/settings.rs#L194) | A review sent from a page names what the page showed: when the app changed since (here "look at it first" set after the page was drawn), nothing is confirmed and the mark stays; the review of what is there now confirms it (review L4). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_review_confirms_only_what_was_shown`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/start_check.rs#L298) | `sam-jail info` names the review fingerprint of what it shows, and `sam-jail review --seen` confirms only that: settings changed again after it was shown are not confirmed, and the app stays blocked (review L4: a review from an out-of-date page confirmed them unseen). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_question_names_the_update_note_that_confirming_accepts`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/app_page/mod.rs#L939) | "Confirm Settings" names the note of an update's needs, which the page hides meanwhile but confirming accepts (review L4). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_review_from_the_page_names_what_it_showed`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/app_page/mod.rs#L948) | The arguments of a review always carry what the page showed, so SAM can refuse it when the app changed since (review L4). | 🆕 new |
| [`only_the_review_clears_look_at_it_first`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/settings.rs#L169) | "Look at it first" (the AppImage's content changed outside SAM) stays through a save of a copy read before it was set, and only the review step clears it (review L3). | 🆕 new |
| [`an_app_changed_outside_sam_starts_only_after_confirmation`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/tamper.rs#L145) | An app whose settings were changed outside SAM does not start until the user has confirmed them; changes SAM makes itself never count as such. | ✅ |
| [`a_changed_record_stays_blocked_until_it_is_confirmed`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/tamper.rs#L184) ⟲ | Regression (audit U-H1): the mark "changed outside SAM" was erased by the next save of the record. | ✅ |
| [`a_late_save_keeps_a_change_made_meanwhile`](../crates/sam-storage/src/lib.rs#L1101) | `update` changes the record as it is on disk now, so work that took a while does not undo a change made meanwhile (audit U-M8: the update check turned a switch back on). | ✅ |
| [`add_reminds_to_confirm_unconfirmed_settings`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/commands.rs#L570) | `add` on the terminal reminds to confirm the settings of an app that is not confirmed yet (it does not start before), and not of one added with --accept. | 🆕 new |
| [`confirming_changed_settings_names_what_they_allow`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/app_page/mod.rs#L1047) | "Confirm Settings" names what the changed settings let the app do before it may start again: full home and X11 written into the record from outside appear in the question (audit G-F12/13: one click confirmed them unseen). | 🆕 new |

**Code:** [`sam-storage/src/lib.rs`](../crates/sam-storage/src/lib.rs), [`sam-runner/src/launch.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/launch.rs), [`sam-runner/src/ops/profiles.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/profiles.rs), [`sam-gtk/src/app_page/render.rs`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/app_page/render.rs)

**Also tried by:** [`tests/gui/app-page-atspi.sh`](../tests/gui/app-page-atspi.sh)

<a id="28"></a>
### 28. Updates use HTTPS only, replace only the AppImage SAM unpacked and unpack what they checked, keep the old version so it can be restored, refuse a changed signing key until you accept it (the yes covers only the file you were shown), and never install a build for another processor.

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`both_forms_of_update_information_are_understood`](../crates/sam-detect/src/update_info.rs#L157) | Both forms of update information (a direct zsync address and GitHub releases) are understood. | ✅ |
| [`update_wildcards_match_only_the_right_build`](../crates/sam-detect/src/update_info.rs#L197) | Wildcards in update information match the right file name and not the build for another processor. | ✅ |
| [`signed_appimage_is_checked`](../crates/sam-detect/tests/signature.rs#L144) | The signature of a signed AppImage is checked with GnuPG: unsigned, valid with the signer's fingerprint and name, invalid after one changed byte. | ✅ |
| [`gnupg_reads_an_appimages_key_and_signature_only_in_a_sandbox`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/gnupg_sandbox.rs#L53) | Gnupg reads an appimages key and signature only in a sandbox. | 🆕 new |
| [`key_changes_are_noticed`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/mod.rs#L996) | An update signed with another key, or not signed at all, is noticed. | ✅ |
| [`an_update_signed_with_another_key_waits_for_consent`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L822) | A new signing key: refused until the person accepts it (audit B24; verification audit 2026-11-19: only a unit test of the comparison showed this). | ✅ |
| [`an_update_that_cannot_be_checked_after_a_checked_signature_waits_for_consent`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_without_gnupg.rs#L58) | An update whose signature cannot be checked waits for a yes when the installed version's signature was checked. | 🆕 new |
| [`sam_updates_itself_only_with_its_release_key`](../crates/sam-runner/src/release_key.rs#L105) | SAM accepts an update of itself only with a valid signature from one of its release keys; unsigned, changed, unchecked or otherwise signed updates are refused (regression: self-update installed any AppImage the update information pointed to). | ✅ |
| [`release_keys_are_full_fingerprints`](../crates/sam-runner/src/release_key.rs#L77) | Every built-in release key is a full fingerprint (40 hex characters), never a short key id that another key could share. | ✅ |
| [`signing_a_file_without_signature_sections_is_refused`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/robustness.rs#L259) | Signing a file that has no AppImage signature sections is refused with a clear message, before GnuPG is asked anything, and the file stays as it was. | ✅ |
| [`without_a_release_key_sam_never_updates_itself`](../crates/sam-runner/src/release_key.rs#L141) | Without a built-in release key, SAM never updates itself, whatever the download looks like. | ✅ |
| [`the_zsync_header_is_read`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1147) | The header of a zsync file is read (file name, size, checksum). | ✅ |
| [`update_download_addresses_are_resolved_and_http_refused`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1171) | Download addresses in zsync files are resolved, and plain-HTTP addresses are refused. | ✅ |
| [`updates_are_downloaded_over_https_only`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1453) | Update downloads use HTTPS only; HTTP and file addresses are refused. | ✅ |
| [`an_update_is_rebuilt_from_the_old_version_and_changed_blocks`](../crates/sam-runner/src/zsync.rs#L507) | An update is rebuilt from the old version plus the changed blocks. | ✅ |
| [`the_rolling_checksum_matches_a_direct_computation`](../crates/sam-runner/src/zsync.rs#L563) | The rolling checksum of the update transfer matches a direct computation at every offset. | ✅ |
| [`refuses_compressed_and_broken_files`](../crates/sam-runner/src/zsync.rs#L579) | Compressed or incomplete zsync files are refused. | ✅ |
| [`undo_puts_the_previous_version_back`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L15) | Undoing an update puts the previous version back. | ✅ |
| [`undoing_an_update_never_moves_files_outside_sams_backup_folder`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/tamper.rs#L89) ⟲ | Regression (found in the test audit): "go back to the earlier version" moved the file a record named as the backup, wherever it was. | ✅ |
| [`block_checksums_match_the_zsync_definition`](../crates/sam-runner/src/zsync.rs#L455) | The block checksums are the ones zsync defines: MD4 of a block padded with zeros to the block size, and the weak sum cut to its last bytes. | ✅ |
| [`the_download_checksum_is_sha1_of_the_whole_file`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1041) | The checksum of a downloaded update is SHA-1 of the whole file, read in pieces; a file larger than one piece gives the same result. | ✅ |
| [`updates_download_only_changes_check_them_and_refuse_tampering`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L212) | Updates download only changes check them and refuse tampering. | ✅ |
| [`the_weak_hashes_stay_in_the_zsync_update_code`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/architecture.rs#L206) | SHA-1 and MD4 are broken as protection against tampering. | ✅ |
| [`an_update_never_comes_over_plain_http`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L734) | Updates come over HTTPS only, wherever the address comes from (audit B22, verification audit 2026-11-19: no test noticed the download's own check gone, and none tried a redirect). | ✅ |
| [`a_download_from_plain_http_is_refused_before_curl_runs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1009) | The download itself refuses an address that is not HTTPS, before curl runs: the check of the update description is one layer, this is the second (verification audit 2026-11-19: no test noticed it gone, as curl then failed with a different error). | ✅ |
| [`an_update_check_of_a_swapped_appimage_asks_no_server`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L1707) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R88): the update check read the update address from whatever file was in the AppImage's place, never compared with what SAM unpacked (only the update itself compared it). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_update_check_hashes_the_whole_file_in_one_read`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1065) | The update check reads an AppImage once and takes both checksums of the whole file from that read, also past the first 8 MiB it keeps for the update information (review B2 S6, R88): a checksum of only the kept part would let a file changed further on pass as the one SAM unpacked. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_update_check_reads_nothing_of_a_file_that_is_not_the_unpacked_one`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1101) | The update check reads nothing of a file that is not a plain file of the size SAM unpacked (review B2 S6, R88, second review): another app could put a link to `/dev/zero`, a named pipe or a huge sparse file in the AppImage's place, and every check (also the one when SAM opens) would read forever. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_app_never_unpacked_is_not_checked_for_updates`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L1777) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R88, second review): an app that was never unpacked (a first unpack that failed) has no record of what SAM unpacked, and the checksum on its record follows whatever file is in the AppImage's place (SAM's change check refreshes it). | 🆕 new |
| [`an_update_that_cannot_be_checked_needs_consent_after_a_valid_signature`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/mod.rs#L1046) | A signed app whose update cannot be checked (GnuPG missing) is not waved through: SAM cannot tell whether the key changed. | ✅ |
| [`an_update_without_a_sandbox_stops_before_downloading`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/unpack.rs#L247) | An update where SAM can build no sandbox stops at once with that reason, before anything is downloaded or asked: the new version could be neither unpacked nor its signature checked there. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_second_key_in_the_file_cannot_borrow_a_trusted_name`](../crates/sam-detect/tests/signature.rs#L179) | The attack of audit finding C1: a file carries a trusted key (here it stands for SAM's release key, which is public) first and the attacker's key second, and is signed with the attacker's key. | ✅ |
| [`a_signature_by_a_subkey_names_its_primary_key`](../crates/sam-detect/tests/signature.rs#L222) | A key whose signatures come from a signing subkey: SAM names the primary key (the fingerprint people publish and SAM compares), not the subkey. | ✅ |
| [`gnupg_names_the_real_signer`](../crates/sam-detect/tests/signature.rs#L245) | The contract with GnuPG (docs/contracts.toml, `signatures-gnupg`) holds with the GnuPG installed here: gpgv accepts a signature by any key in its keyring, and its status output names the key that really signed. | ✅ |
| [`sam_refuses_a_forged_update_that_carries_its_release_key`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/self_update_signature.rs#L89) | SAM installs an update of itself only when it was really signed with its release key. | ✅ |
| [`sam_updates_itself_from_its_own_copy_even_if_the_file_is_replaced`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/self_update_signature.rs#L281) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R87): SAM's update of itself read its AppImage in the person's folder while it worked: the update information, the old blocks of a partial download (mapped into memory, where a file shortened meanwhile crashes SAM) and the running version's signature. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_second_update_of_sam_is_refused_while_one_runs`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/self_update_signature.rs#L350) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R95): two updates of SAM at once (the window and the command line) shared their download and backup folders and deleted each other's files, the backup of the old SAM among them. | 🆕 new |
| [`one_update_of_sam_at_a_time`](../crates/sam-runner/src/app_lock.rs#L293) | One update of SAM at a time: a second is refused at once, and free again once the first ends. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_signer_is_what_gpgv_reports_and_nothing_unclear_counts`](../crates/sam-detect/src/signature.rs#L565) | The signer is read from gpgv's own report of the signature it checked: the primary key, or the signing key when no primary is given. | ✅ |
| [`primary_keys_are_counted_without_their_subkeys`](../crates/sam-detect/src/signature.rs#L626) | Primary keys are counted from GnuPG's listing; subkeys are not keys of their own (a signer with a signing subkey carries one key). | ✅ |
| [`the_contract_material_names_key_b`](../crates/sam-detect/src/signature.rs#L642) | The fixed test material of the GnuPG contract is what its comments say: key B's fingerprint is the one recorded. | ✅ |
| [`a_helper_program_is_stopped_at_its_time_limit`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L954) | A helper program that hangs is stopped at the time limit instead of hanging SAM (GnuPG reads keys from untrusted AppImages), and a program that prints more than SAM keeps neither blocks nor floods SAM's memory. | ✅ |
| [`an_update_writes_through_no_planted_link_and_keeps_the_sandbox`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L373) ⟲ | Regressions (audit U-H4, U-H3): updates were downloaded next to the AppImage under a guessable name (`.<name>.sam-download`) with tools that follow links, so an app with write access to that folder (often Downloads) could plant a link there and SAM wrote the update through it into any file of the person. | ✅ |
| [`going_back_with_an_unchanged_file_name_keeps_both_versions`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L468) ⟲ | Regression (audit U-H5): when the new version had the same file name as the old one, going back moved the current file onto the kept old version (destroying it), then "restored" the new file and reported that the app was back. | ✅ |
| [`a_download_folder_is_fresh_private_and_sams_own`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/maintain.rs#L2519) | Updates are downloaded into a fresh folder inside SAM's data folder that only SAM's user can enter (audit U-H4): never the AppImage's folder, never one with leftovers. | ✅ |
| [`a_move_across_drives_never_writes_through_a_link`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/maintain.rs#L2539) | Across file systems (a download in SAM's folder, the AppImage on another drive) a move is a copy: into a new file with the same permissions, never through a link planted at the target and never over a file that is there (audit U-H4: `fs::copy` followed links). | ✅ |
| [`an_older_signed_version_is_not_installed_as_an_update`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L527) ⟲ | Regression (audit U-M6): whoever controls the download could hand out an older, validly signed release (with holes fixed since) as the update: any file other than the installed one counted as newer. | ✅ |
| [`an_older_signed_version_is_recognized`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/mod.rs#L955) | An update signed with the same key but earlier than the installed version is an older version; later, equal, another key, unsigned or without a time is not judged here. | ✅ |
| [`a_signing_time_that_cannot_be_read_is_noticed`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/mod.rs#L1077) | The same key on both versions, but one signing time unknown (a record from before SAM kept the time, or a time gpgv did not report): SAM cannot tell whether the update is older. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_older_signed_version_is_refused_when_the_record_has_no_signing_time`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L616) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R94): the refusal of an older version needs both signing times, and a record whose signature was checked before SAM kept the time (before 2026-11-18) was compared as it was, so a server could hand such an app an older release signed with the same key. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_signing_time_that_cannot_be_read_lets_no_older_release_through`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_signing_time.rs#L45) | When SAM cannot read when the installed version or the download was signed, it cannot tell an older release signed with the same key from a newer one, and nothing older gets through on that guess. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_signing_time_is_read_from_the_one_good_signature`](../crates/sam-detect/src/signature.rs#L608) | The signing time is the one good signature's own (seconds since 1970); nothing unclear gives a time. | ✅ |
| [`a_hostile_update_description_cannot_crash_or_stall_sam`](../crates/sam-runner/src/zsync.rs#L808) | A description from a hostile server cannot crash SAM or keep it busy (audit U-M7): an absurd length is refused, a block count that would overflow the size computation is refused instead of wrapping, and thousands of blocks whose quick checksum matches a stretch of identical bytes end at the time limit, with those blocks left for downloading. | ✅ |
| [`kept_data_goes_back_only_to_its_own_appimage`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/tamper.rs#L237) ⟲ | Regression (audit I-F5): an id is the app's name plus four hex digits of its file's checksum, so a crafted AppImage can get the id of a removed app whose data was kept, and it inherited that private home. | ✅ |
| [`changed_zsync_controls_never_crash_planning_or_assembly`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/fuzz_targets.rs#L224) | Changed zsync control files are refused, or planned and assembled within their limits, without a panic: the parser, the block plan, the missing ranges and the assembly all see them. | ✅ |
| [`an_appimage_swapped_before_an_update_is_not_updated_into_its_sandbox`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L969) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, U-1): an update trusted whatever file was in the AppImage's place. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_update_unpacks_the_checked_download_even_if_the_placed_file_is_swapped`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L1040) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, U-1): after putting the checked download in the AppImage's folder, SAM read it from there again to unpack it. | 🆕 new |
| [`going_back_refuses_a_current_version_swapped_outside_sam`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L150) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, U-1): going back kept the current file as "the newer version" without looking at it, and going forward again unpacked it as SAM's own work, without the review a replaced file needs. | 🆕 new |
| [`going_back_to_a_version_kept_without_its_checksum_needs_a_review`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L188) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, U-1): a version kept by an earlier SAM was moved out of the AppImage's folder without being checked, so it may not be what SAM unpacked then. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_update_never_takes_a_published_name_that_is_not_an_appimage_name`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L1101) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, U-2): the update server chose the new file's name in the AppImage's folder, and SAM made it executable. | 🆕 new |
| [`only_a_plain_appimage_name_is_taken_from_an_update`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/maintain.rs#L2397) | An update keeps its published name only when that is a plain AppImage name no other file has: the update server chooses it, and a name like `sudo` in ~/.local/bin would replace a command outside any sandbox (review 2026-11-23, U-2). | 🆕 new |
| [`an_update_compares_keys_even_when_the_installed_signature_was_never_checked`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L1137) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, U-3): an app added while GnuPG was missing has its signature recorded as "not checked", and every key comparison needed a checked record, so an update signed with another key was installed without a question even after GnuPG was installed. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_signature_that_was_never_checked_lets_no_key_through`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/mod.rs#L1024) | A signature recorded as "not checked" (GnuPG was missing) lets no key through: once the new file's signature can be read, whatever it is (another key, none, a bad one) counts as a change; both unchecked is no news (review 2026-11-23, U-3). | 🆕 new |
| [`going_back_keeps_a_copy_of_the_newer_version_no_other_app_can_change`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L211) ⟲ | Regression (review check 2026-11-23, U-1): going back moved the current file from the AppImage's folder into SAM's backup folder and checked it there. | 🆕 new |
| [`going_back_keeps_the_files_modes`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L248) | Going back keeps each file's own mode: neither the version put back nor the newer one kept in SAM's folder becomes writable for others (mutation run 2026-11-24: a mode of 0777 went unnoticed). | 🆕 new |
| [`going_back_to_a_kept_version_changed_since_needs_a_review`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L265) | A version SAM checked when it kept it, but changed since, asks for a review when SAM goes back to it (mutation run 2026-11-24: a recorded checksum alone could count as checked). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_failed_going_back_leaves_both_versions_where_they_were`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L286) | When the earlier version cannot be put back (here: it cannot be read), going back fails and leaves both versions where they were: the newer one in place and the earlier one in SAM's backup folder, also when both have the same file name (mutation run 2026-11-24: losing either went unnoticed). | 🆕 new |
| [`going_back_works_when_the_update_could_not_be_unpacked`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L1238) ⟲ | Regression (review check 2026-11-23, U-1): the way back is recorded before the new version is unpacked, so that the old one can be put back when unpacking fails (audit U-L2). | 🆕 new |
| [`an_update_that_cannot_place_the_new_version_changes_nothing`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L1380) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R89): an update deleted the earlier backup and the person's AppImage before it copied the new version in. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_update_whose_record_cannot_be_saved_changes_nothing`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L1396) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R89): when the new version is in place but its record cannot be saved (here: the records' folder is read-only), the update undoes its steps: the old AppImage, the record and the earlier backup stay as they were, for a new name and for the same name (then the record is saved first and one rename replaces the file). | 🆕 new |
| [`an_interrupted_going_back_never_loses_the_earlier_version`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L349) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R86): when putting the earlier version in place failed and putting the current one back failed too (a full disk: two failing copies), the earlier version stayed at `.going-back` and the newer copy at the backup's name. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_going_back_whose_record_cannot_be_saved_changes_nothing`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L390) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R86, R89): going back removed the current file before the earlier version was in place and recorded. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_go_back_stopped_midway_still_lets_the_app_go_back_and_update`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L437) | Review of the fix for R86: a go back that SAM was stopped in (killed, a power loss) left the earlier version at `.going-back`, and from then on every go back and update refused. | 🆕 new |
| [`leftover_incoming_files_of_the_app_are_removed_and_nothing_else`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L492) | Review of the fix for R89: a SAM stopped while writing the new version left a hidden, executable copy of the app in the person's folder for good. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_same_name_go_back_stopped_before_its_record_was_saved_loses_nothing`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L549) | Second review of the fix for R86: with the same name for both versions (the common case), going back wrote the newer copy over the earlier version's kept name before the record was saved, so a stop while copying left a state that blocked every later go back. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_public_copy_of_the_earlier_version_never_makes_sam_delete_its_own`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L603) | Second review of the fix for R86: a `.going-back` left by an older SAM was deleted as a second copy when the app's current file had the same content. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_go_back_stopped_after_placing_under_a_new_name_goes_on`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L630) | Second review of the fix for R86: with different names, a go back stopped after the earlier version took its name but before the record was saved left a complete copy there, and every later go back refused ("exists again"). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_same_name_go_back_stopped_after_its_record_was_saved_is_finished`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L664) | Third review of the fix for R86: a same-name go back stopped after its record was saved but before the earlier version took the app's place left that version named by no record; the next go back removed the placed copy as a leftover, "went back" to the newer one, and deleted SAM's copy of the earlier version. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_update_stopped_after_its_record_was_saved_is_finished_first`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L741) | Third review of the fix for R89: an update stopped after its record was saved but before the new version was in place (or unpacked) left the old file with a way back to itself, and the next update said another program had changed it. | 🆕 new |
| [`removing_or_relocating_an_app_removes_its_incoming_leftovers`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L810) | Third review of the fix for R89: removing the sandbox or relocating the app left this app's half-written incoming copies in the AppImage's folder for good. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_stopped_update_whose_own_copy_changed_is_not_finished`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L832) | A stopped update is finished only from SAM's own copy as it was checked: one changed since is never put in place. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_version_being_put_in_place_survives_relocating`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update.rs#L908) | Reviews of the fix for R86: a same-name go back stopped after its record was saved keeps the earlier version only in SAM's copy that the record's mark names. | 🆕 new |
| [`signing_succeeds_only_with_the_named_fingerprint`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/robustness.rs#L287) | `dev sign-appimage` (the release scripts sign with it) signs with the key named by its full fingerprint and says so; when the signature it made does not check out as that fingerprint's, here because the key was named by its shorter id, it refuses instead of reporting success. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_full_download_stops_at_the_published_length_whatever_the_server_sends`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1212) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, U-5): the size limit of a full download was left to curl's `--max-filesize`, which before curl 8.4 does nothing when the server names no length, so a server could stream into the home folder for an hour. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_mapped_file_is_unmapped_when_dropped`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1336) | A mapping of the installed version is undone when dropped: the window runs for days and maps a whole AppImage at every update (mutation run 2026-11-24). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_failed_download_says_why`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1312) | A failed download says why, in words, also after up to 64 KiB of other error output (2000 bytes here, more than a cut to 1 KiB would keep): curl's last line is kept (mutation run 2026-11-24: losing it went unnoticed, leaving "download failed:" with no reason). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_whole_answer_is_never_cut_off_and_handed_on_as_complete`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1363) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, U-7): an answer longer than SAM reads was cut off and handed on as if complete, so a GitHub release description over 512 KiB failed as "GitHub's answer" every time. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_release_with_hundreds_of_files_is_read_whole`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1376) | A release with 600 files: each file's entry carries its uploader, so the description is well over 512 KiB (review 2026-11-23, U-7). | 🆕 new |
| [`release_files_for_this_processor_come_first`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1427) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, U-8): with a pattern that matches the builds for every processor, the release's first file was taken, so an x86-64 computer could get the ARM build. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_description_with_tiny_or_countless_blocks_is_refused_before_allocating`](../crates/sam-runner/src/zsync.rs#L594) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, U-6): `Blocksize: 1` and the shortest checksums made a description of 64 MiB (the most SAM fetches) list about 16 million blocks, and SAM allocated some 2 GB for them before giving up on the delta. | 🆕 new |
| [`blocks_are_reused_in_pairs_when_the_description_asks_for_it`](../crates/sam-runner/src/zsync.rs#L660) | With `seq_matches` 2 (zsyncmake's usual choice, with shorter stored checksums) a block is taken only together with the next one: an unchanged file is still reused whole, also in zsyncmake's own short format, and a block whose next one changed is downloaded (mutation run 2026-11-24: the check of the next block could fail every time unnoticed, so everything was downloaded). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_short_last_block_is_reused_only_when_both_checksums_match`](../crates/sam-runner/src/zsync.rs#L638) | The short last block is found at the end of the old file, and only when both its checksums match: a quick checksum alone takes no block (mutation run 2026-11-24). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_server_that_sends_more_than_announced_cannot_fill_the_disk`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L1493) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, U-5): a full download stopped at the published length only through curl's `--max-filesize`, which before curl 8.4 does nothing when the server names no length: a server could stream into the home folder for an hour. | 🆕 new |
| [`an_update_built_for_another_processor_is_not_installed`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L1436) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, U-8): an update did not look at the processor the new file is built for. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_whole_update_download_stops_at_one_deadline`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_partial.rs#L147) | A slow server: eight ranges of 16 KiB at 8 KiB a second take about twelve seconds, the whole file two minutes. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_server_that_ignores_ranges_gets_one_range_request_then_the_whole_file`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_partial.rs#L104) | A server that ignores Range sends the whole file to every range request. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_range_answered_with_more_bytes_stops_at_once`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1246) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R90): a range request answered with the whole file (a server that ignores ranges) kept the first bytes and read the rest to its end, so each of up to 300 ranges was a whole download. | 🆕 new |
| [`assembling_an_update_never_writes_through_a_planted_link`](../crates/sam-runner/src/zsync.rs#L772) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R91): `assemble` opened its target with create and truncate, following links, unlike a whole download. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_block_before_a_short_last_block_is_reused_in_pairs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/zsync.rs#L707) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R84): with `seq_matches` 2 a block is taken only together with the next one, and when that next one was a short last block, a whole block was compared and never matched, so the block before it was downloaded at every update. | 🆕 new |
| [`planning_stops_at_its_limit_also_when_blocks_match_off_the_checkpoints`](../crates/sam-runner/src/zsync.rs#L733) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R90): planning looked at the clock only at offsets that are a multiple of 64 KiB. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_whole_file_download_gets_only_the_time_left`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_partial.rs#L193) | The whole file gets only the time left of the update's limit, never an hour of its own (review B2 S6, R90). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_time_limit_is_named_only_when_it_ended_the_download`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs#L1285) | The update's time limit is named in minutes when it is whole minutes (an hour for SAM's updates), in seconds otherwise; and only curl's own time-out is named as the limit, so a download that fails for another reason near the end keeps its reason (review B2 S6, R90). | 🆕 new |
| [`consent_to_a_new_key_covers_only_the_file_that_was_shown`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/update_server.rs#L1572) ⟲ | Regression (review 2026-11-23, U-4): "Update Anyway" ran the update a second time with a blanket "accept any key change". | 🆕 new |
| [`going_back_puts_the_earlier_version_only_into_the_apps_folder`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/tamper.rs#L111) ⟲ | Regression (audit U-L10): going back took the destination from the record unchecked. | 🆕 new |
| [`declining_an_update_deletes_the_download_that_waited`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/commands.rs#L730) | `update APP --decline-key-change` (the window's answer "no" to an update signed differently) deletes the download that waited for that answer, and only that one; the app stays as it was. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_download_never_answered_is_deleted_after_a_week`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/maintain.rs#L2373) | A download that waited for an answer about an update for more than a week (the question was never answered) is deleted when updates are checked; a younger one stays for its answer. | 🆕 new |
| [`only_an_https_release_page_is_opened`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/window.rs#L2012) | The release page of SAM's own update opens only when it is an https page: another scheme could open a local file or start a program through its handler (audit G-F12/13). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_note_names_new_needs_the_settings_lack`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/profiles.rs#L731) | The note names the needs as before the one list (GPU, audio, network, X11, folders), only those newly asked for and missing. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_note_leaves_out_a_need_the_old_recommendation_already_asked_for`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/profiles.rs#L759) | A need the old recommendation already asked for is not new: when the person switched it off (the GPU) or gave less (the downloads folder read-only instead of read and write), the note after an update or Unpack Again does not name it again as if the app newly asked for it. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_switch_change_unrelated_to_the_note_keeps_it`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/profiles.rs#L638) | A switch the note does not name, changed on the app's page or the command line, keeps the note: the person has not seen it by that (review L5; before, any change cleared it). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_group_change_from_the_permissions_page_keeps_the_note`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/profiles.rs#L649) | A change for a group of apps on the permissions page keeps the note of what it does not grant (review L5). | 🆕 new |
| [`granting_exactly_the_requested_need_removes_it_from_the_note`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/profiles.rs#L663) | Granting what the note asked for takes exactly that need off the note; the others stay. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_folder_need_is_granted_only_at_the_level_asked`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/profiles.rs#L676) | A folder need counts as granted only at the level the recommendation asks for: read access does not grant read and write. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_note_kept_through_a_change_goes_with_got_it`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/profiles.rs#L695) | The note kept through a change goes with its own "Got It" (the app's review), even with nothing granted. | 🆕 new |
| [`reading_the_app_again_keeps_an_unseen_need`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/profiles.rs#L710) | Reading the app again (an update, Unpack Again) adds what it newly asks for and keeps what an earlier update asked for and nobody saw; before, the new list replaced the note, so a second Unpack Again emptied it. | 🆕 new |

**Code:** [`sam-runner/src/updates.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/updates.rs), [`sam-runner/src/zsync.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/zsync.rs), [`sam-runner/src/release_key.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/release_key.rs), [`sam-detect/src/update_info.rs`](../crates/sam-detect/src/update_info.rs), [`sam-detect/src/signature.rs`](../crates/sam-detect/src/signature.rs)

<a id="29"></a>
### 29. SAM notices AppImages that were moved or changed.

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`a_swapped_appimage_does_not_start_until_it_is_confirmed`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/changed_appimage.rs#L16) | A swapped appimage does not start until it is confirmed. | ✅ |
| [`what_is_unpacked_is_what_sam_checked_even_if_the_file_is_swapped`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/swap_during_unpack.rs#L50) | What is unpacked is what sam checked even if the file is swapped. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_swapped_appimage_stays_marked_when_a_switch_changes`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/changed_appimage.rs#L74) ⟲ | Regression (audit U-H1, Part 5): changing any switch cleared the mark of a swapped AppImage file, so it started without the person looking at it. | ✅ |
| [`a_swapped_appimage_is_marked_also_after_the_change_check`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/changed_appimage.rs#L114) ⟲ | Regression (audit U-H2): "Check all AppImages for changes" (or moving the file with relocate) recorded the swapped file's checksum, so the following "Unpack Again" compared the file with itself: the swapped content ran with the old app's permissions, unmarked, and its signature was not checked again. | ✅ |
| [`a_swapped_appimage_file_is_marked_and_old_records_stay_unchanged`](../crates/sam-core/src/model.rs#L793) | An AppImage file swapped outside SAM shows as changed outside SAM; while the mark is off it is not written, so every record saved before it existed keeps its checksum (no app looks changed after an update). | ✅ |
| [`a_changed_appimage_file_is_noticed`](../crates/sam-intake/src/fingerprint.rs#L158) | A changed AppImage file is noticed by its fingerprint. | ✅ |
| [`a_moved_appimage_is_recognized_by_its_content`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/settings.rs#L411) | When a sandboxed app's AppImage is moved to another searched folder, SAM recognizes it by its content and offers to follow it. | ✅ |
| [`a_swapped_appimage_moved_elsewhere_is_not_taken_for_the_apps_own_file`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/changed_appimage.rs#L190) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R96): another app swaps an AppImage, the change check records the swapped file as the file last seen, and the app moves the swapped file elsewhere. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_moved_appimage_added_again_is_followed_with_its_marks`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/changed_appimage.rs#L256) | An AppImage moved to another folder and added there is followed, as before: the app keeps its sandbox, its unpacked checksum and its marks. | 🆕 new |
| [`following_a_moved_appimage_keeps_a_change_made_while_it_was_checked`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/changed_appimage.rs#L309) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R96): adding a moved AppImage read the app's record before copying and checking the file, which can take a while, and saved that copy: a change made meanwhile, such as a switch changed in the window, was undone. | 🆕 new |

**Code:** [`sam-intake/src/fingerprint.rs`](../crates/sam-intake/src/fingerprint.rs), [`sam-runner/src/ops/maintain.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/maintain.rs)

## G. Managing

<a id="30"></a>
### 30. Removing an app deletes only SAM's own folders, never your files.

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`removing_a_sandbox_never_deletes_outside_sams_unpack_folder`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/tamper.rs#L34) ⟲ | Regression: the unpack folder in a record was only checked with Path::starts_with, which does not resolve "..": "extracted/../../victim" passed, and removing the sandbox deleted the victim folder. | ✅ |
| [`a_record_with_a_climbing_id_reaches_nothing`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/tamper.rs#L66) | A record whose id climbs out of SAM's folders cannot be loaded, so no operation can reach the folders that id would name. | ✅ |
| [`records_with_unsafe_or_mismatched_ids_are_not_loaded`](../crates/sam-storage/src/lib.rs#L1170) ⟲ | Regression (found in the test audit): a record changed outside SAM with an id like "../../.ssh" would have pointed the folders SAM deletes on removal (private home, logs) outside SAM's own folders. | ✅ |
| [`removing_with_keep_my_data_keeps_the_private_home_and_without_deletes_it`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/settings.rs#L369) | Removing a sandbox with "keep my data" keeps the app's private home; without it, the home is deleted. | ✅ |
| [`kept_data_goes_back_to_the_unpacked_file_after_a_swap_was_seen`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/changed_appimage.rs#L366) ⟲ | Regression (review B2 S6, R96): "keep my data" recorded the file last seen as the owner of the kept data. | 🆕 new |
| [`removing_a_sandbox_while_its_app_runs_is_refused`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/settings.rs#L220) | A running app keeps its sandbox: removing it is refused until the app is closed (a launch holds a lock in the app's runtime folder). | ✅ |
| [`names_become_safe_identifiers`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L1061) | Names become safe identifiers made of lowercase letters, digits and dashes. | ✅ |
| [`an_apps_icon_is_kept_in_sams_icon_folder_and_goes_with_it`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/settings.rs#L389) | The app's icon is copied into SAM's own icon folder, named after the app, and goes with its sandbox; nothing lands in the folder SAM was started in. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_extraction_folder_is_never_the_root_or_the_home_folder`](../crates/sam-core/src/config.rs#L761) | The extraction folder is a folder of its own: never the root, never the home folder itself (however it is written), never relative and never with `..`. | 🆕 new |

**Code:** [`sam-runner/src/ops/maintain.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/maintain.rs), [`sam-storage/src/lib.rs`](../crates/sam-storage/src/lib.rs), [`sam-core/src/util.rs`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs)

### 31. Disk space per app, and freeing space.

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`freeing_space_deletes_the_unpacked_copy_and_unpacking_restores_it`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/space.rs#L13) | Freeing space deletes the unpacked copy, and unpacking again brings the app back. | ✅ |
| [`sizes_beyond_a_tebibyte_stay_in_tebibytes`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L1115) | Sizes are shown in the largest unit up to TiB, also for sizes far beyond (a sparse file can claim any size): no unit past the table. | 🆕 new |

| Test | Promise | Result |

| [`uninstall_refuses_folders_that_are_not_sams`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/uninstall.rs#L123) | Uninstalling refuses to delete a folder that is not SAM's, such as the home folder. | ✅ |

<a id="33"></a>
### 33. The doctor check finds problems with the system or an app.

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`a_setuid_bubblewrap_is_told_from_a_normal_one`](../crates/sam-core/src/host.rs#L1279) | A setuid Bubblewrap is told from a normal one by its file's setuid bit: only a setuid one works where user namespaces are switched off. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_bubblewrap_option_check_counts_only_the_option_itself`](../crates/sam-runner/src/contracts.rs#L794) | The quick check for a Bubblewrap option asks SAM's one option test: a look-alike that merely contains the name, help on standard error only or a failing help do not count, and no Bubblewrap is "not here" (stand-ins; the real one is checked by `sam-jail doctor --contracts` and on CI). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_user_namespace_knobs_are_read_as_meant`](../crates/sam-core/src/host.rs#L1296) | The kernel's user namespace knobs are read as they are meant: "1" is on, "0" off, a missing file unknown (the AppArmor restriction of Ubuntu 24.04 and later included). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_landlock_version_is_the_kernels`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/host_probe.rs#L42) | The Landlock version SAM reads is the kernel's: none where Landlock is not active, and at least the version this kernel brings where it is (a wrong one turns layers on that do not exist, or switches off the check that Landlock fences abstract sockets). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_kernel_release_gives_the_least_landlock_version`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/host_probe.rs#L61) | The kernel release gives the least landlock version. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_landlock_answer_of_zero_is_no_landlock`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/host_probe.rs#L90) | Where Landlock's version query answers 0 (no version at all; a filter SAM runs under that fakes success), SAM counts no Landlock: it must never report or plan on a protection layer that is not there. | 🆕 new |
| [`missing_bubblewrap_names_the_package_to_install`](../crates/sam-runner/src/bwrap.rs#L55) | Without Bubblewrap, the message says which package to install. | ✅ |
| [`disabled_user_namespaces_are_explained_not_called_missing`](../crates/sam-runner/src/bwrap.rs#L69) | When the system forbids user namespaces, the message explains that and never claims Bubblewrap is missing: the user already has it, and an "install bubblewrap" hint would send them the wrong way. | ✅ |
| [`setuid_bubblewrap_works_without_user_namespaces`](../crates/sam-runner/src/bwrap.rs#L96) | A setuid Bubblewrap works without unprivileged user namespaces, so SAM does not refuse it. | ✅ |
| [`reaches_the_session_bus`](../crates/sam-runner/src/dbus_check.rs#L275) | SAM's own bus check reaches a session bus. | ✅ |
| [`the_bus_check_message_has_the_correct_wire_format`](../crates/sam-runner/src/dbus_check.rs#L325) | SAM's own D-Bus message for the bus check has the correct wire format. | ✅ |
| [`the_built_in_contract_map_parses`](../crates/sam-runner/src/contracts.rs#L741) | The built in contract map parses. | ✅ |
| [`every_contract_probe_is_known`](../crates/sam-runner/src/contracts.rs#L822) | Every contract probe is known. | ✅ |
| [`the_login_files_check_looks_for_shadow`](../crates/sam-runner/src/contracts.rs#L707) | The quick check for login and admin files holds where /etc/shadow is, names what it found, and breaks where it is not (then the sandbox tests would look for the secrets in the wrong place). | ✅ |
| [`a_missing_portal_interface_breaks_the_portal_contract`](../crates/sam-runner/src/contracts.rs#L918) | A missing portal interface breaks the portal contract. | ✅ |
| [`the_quick_checks_notice_a_broken_or_missing_tool`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/contracts.rs#L48) | The quick checks notice a broken or missing tool. | ✅ |
| [`a_broken_contract_fails_a_test_with_its_name_and_playbook`](../crates/sam-runner/src/contracts.rs#L834) | A broken contract fails a test with its name and playbook. | ✅ |
| [`a_broken_contract_ends_with_not_passed`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/exit_codes.rs#L239) | A broken contract (here a Bubblewrap that cannot build a sandbox) ends `doctor --contracts` with 3. | ✅ |
| [`doctor_ends_with_what_it_reports`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/exit_codes.rs#L134) | `doctor` and `doctor --contracts` end with 3 exactly when they report a failed check or a broken contract, and with 0 otherwise. | ✅ |
| [`the_install_hint_fits_the_distribution`](../crates/sam-core/src/host.rs#L1369) | The install hint names this distribution's package manager, also on derivatives (by `ID_LIKE`), and none where SAM does not know it (audit T2). | ✅ |
| [`the_apparmor_advice_keeps_the_restriction_for_other_programs`](../crates/sam-core/src/host.rs#L1354) | Where AppArmor restricts user namespaces, the advice names the restricted profile for Bubblewrap alone and what it costs, and warns against the ways that lift the restriction for every program (audit A3). | ✅ |
| [`diagnostics_say_when_landlock_cannot_fence_abstract_sockets`](../crates/sam-runner/src/doctor.rs#L1006) | Review K-1: before Linux 6.12 Landlock cannot fence abstract sockets, and diagnostics say so (it read "available" as if all was well); from 6.12 on it is fine. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_bubblewrap_status_says_what_is_missing_and_nothing_else`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs#L9129) | What SAM says about Bubblewrap names what is wrong, and only that. | 🆕 new |
| [`diagnostics_warn_about_what_this_session_lacks`](../crates/sam-runner/src/doctor.rs#L1033) | Diagnostics warn about a missing Wayland socket on a Wayland session and only mention it elsewhere; they list the GPU devices found, and warn when there are none. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_wayland_session_without_its_socket_breaks_the_wayland_contract`](../crates/sam-runner/src/contracts.rs#L758) | A Wayland session whose compositor socket SAM cannot find breaks the Wayland contract (the sandbox tests would otherwise skip every window check); on an X11 session the socket is simply not there. | 🆕 new |

**Code:** [`sam-runner/src/doctor.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/doctor.rs), [`sam-runner/src/bwrap.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/bwrap.rs), [`sam-runner/src/dbus_check.rs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/dbus_check.rs), [`sam-core/src/host.rs`](../crates/sam-core/src/host.rs)

<a id="34"></a>
### 34. Privacy: SAM sends nothing on its own, and a bug report never contains your name, home path, addresses or secrets.

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`given_environment_values_are_hidden_in_the_shown_command`](../crates/sam-core/src/sandbox.rs#L383) | Values of variables a person gave the app never reach the shown command; the names and SAM's own variables stay. | ✅ |
| [`bug_reports_hide_the_user_name_and_home_path`](../crates/sam-runner/src/doctor.rs#L987) | A bug report never contains the user's name or home path: both are replaced before the text is shown for copying. | ✅ |
| [`bug_reports_hide_names_addresses_and_secrets`](../crates/sam-runner/src/doctor.rs#L1128) | Bug reports hide everything that identifies the user or gives access: a home folder with spaces, other users' folders, short user names, the computer's name, email addresses, logins and tokens in links, IP addresses, and the values of environment variables given to the app. | ✅ |
| [`bug_reports_hide_secrets_in_every_spelling`](../crates/sam-runner/src/doctor.rs#L1086) | Secrets whose name goes on after the known word (`GITHUB_TOKEN_2`, `password-hash`, `secret2`), with spaces or quotes around the value, and empty values (mutation run P14: these forms were untested). | ✅ |
| [`a_bug_report_is_complete_and_redacted`](../crates/sam-runner/src/doctor.rs#L1064) | A whole bug report (the system part) is produced and redacted: it names SAM's version and never the home folder or user name. | ✅ |
| [`update_checks_stay_off_unless_switched_on`](../crates/sam-core/src/config.rs#L543) | SAM contacts no update source on its own: the daily check when the window opens is off until the user switches it on. | ✅ |
| [`the_machine_id_reaches_only_apps_with_dbus`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs#L4045) | The machine ID identifies this computer; an app sees it only with D-Bus, which needs it, never without. | ✅ |
| [`bug_reports_hide_tokens_in_every_shape`](../crates/sam-runner/src/doctor.rs#L1204) | Bug reports also hide what gives access in other shapes (audit U-L5): a token after `#` or inside a link's path, `Authorization` and cookie lines, and named secrets in settings and logs. | ✅ |
| [`a_log_cannot_break_out_of_its_code_block`](../crates/sam-runner/src/doctor.rs#L1276) | An app's log cannot end the report's code block and add text of its own after it: the fence is always longer than any run of backticks inside. | ✅ |

## H. The window

<a id="35"></a>
### 35. The overview of all apps, with clear status badges.

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`profile_change_clears_broken_launch`](../crates/sam-core/src/model.rs#L775) | Changing an app's settings clears the record of a failed start, so the app is no longer shown as broken. | ✅ |
| [`apps_with_compatibility_risk_are_marked_until_they_start`](../crates/sam-core/src/model.rs#L873) | An app with compatibility risk carries a warning badge until it has started successfully once. | ✅ |
| [`apps_are_found_by_name_or_id_and_ambiguous_names_are_refused`](../crates/sam-storage/src/lib.rs#L1223) | Apps are found by name or ID, and an ambiguous name is refused. | ✅ |
| [`nested_items_keep_their_text`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/guide.rs#L351) | The user guide keeps the text of a list item that has a nested list below it. | ✅ |
| [`links_to_other_pages_open_on_github`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/guide.rs#L375) | Links in the guide to other documentation pages open on GitHub, since relative file names lead nowhere inside the window (regression: the link to "Terminal programs" pointed to security.md and did nothing). | ✅ |
| [`every_state_risk_and_severity_is_shown_in_its_own_words`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/src/output.rs#L434) | Every state, risk level and warning severity is shown in its own words: `list`, `info`, `add`, `set` and `evaluate` tell the user an app is Unsafe, Dangerous or lacks its protection only through these. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_long_name_is_cut_to_its_column`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/src/output.rs#L531) | A name longer than its column is cut to the column with "…", so an app cannot push SAM's real risk and state out of their columns with a long name of its own (a fake "Harmless  Ready" in the name would line up under them). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_long_app_name_cannot_push_risk_and_state_out_of_their_columns`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/human_views.rs#L324) | A long name with columns of its own cannot push SAM's real risk and state out of their columns in `list`, nor the permission tags in `group list`: the name is cut to its column. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_marked_state_decides_the_badge`](../crates/sam-core/src/model.rs#L989) | A record SAM marked broken, stale or unsafe shows that badge whatever else is true of it: a re-unpacking that failed keeps the old unpacked folder, a changed AppImage file keeps its record, and an app that asks for administrator rights stays Unsafe even after a start that failed in the sandbox setup (which alone shows Broken). | 🆕 new |
| [`every_badge_is_named_for_people_and_scripts`](../crates/sam-core/src/model.rs#L1027) | Every badge has its own name, the one the command line shows and the JSON interface gives scripts in `badge_label` next to the code in `badge`: a warning badge never reads as another or as nothing. | 🆕 new |
| [`every_isolation_verdict_says_why`](../crates/sam-core/src/model.rs#L1073) | Each isolation verdict tells the person why, in words of its own (shown as the app's warning and in `info`). | 🆕 new |
| [`list_filters_and_sorts_the_apps`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/commands.rs#L475) | `list` finds apps by name or by id alone, by badge and by category, and sorts by the last start or by when they were added. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_name_an_app_chose_cannot_take_over_another_apps_query`](../crates/sam-storage/src/lib.rs#L1256) ⟲ | Regression (audit U-L7): the name an AppImage gives itself came first, so one added later calling itself "firefox" was started for `sam-jail run firefox` instead of "Firefox Web Browser", with its own permissions. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_header_is_not_green_while_a_protection_layer_was_missing`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/app_page/mod.rs#L1073) | The header's "Sandboxed" is green only while the last start had every protection layer; with one missing it is amber and names the layer (audit G-F15: it stayed green). | 🆕 new |

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`texts_without_a_translation_come_back_with_their_values`](../crates/sam-core/src/i18n.rs#L322) | Texts without a translation come back with their values. | ✅ |
| [`the_command_line_speaks_german_when_asked`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/language.rs#L18) | The command line speaks german when asked. | ✅ |
| [`the_helpers_line_speaks_the_persons_language`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/launch_hygiene.rs#L457) | The line naming what SAM's helper sets for the app speaks the person's language, in `sam-jail command` and in the launch log, like SAM's other texts (it was English only). | 🆕 new |
| [`every_translated_language_can_be_chosen`](../crates/sam-core/src/i18n.rs#L230) | Without a translation, texts come back as written, placeholders are filled in by name (so a translation may reorder them), and the plural form follows the number. | ✅ |
| [`the_language_is_the_system_or_one_sam_speaks`](../crates/sam-core/src/config.rs#L556) | The window's language is empty (follow the system) or one of the languages SAM speaks; anything else is refused, and a wrong value in the file is reset to the system's language. | ✅ |
| [`the_chosen_language_and_the_systems_are_kept_apart`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/i18n.rs#L197) | Choosing a language sets it for the window and remembers the system's; going back to the system restores exactly what it had, also after several restarts. | ✅ |
| [`apps_keep_the_system_language_whatever_sams_window_shows`](../crates/sam-sandbox/src/lib.rs#L5237) | An app started from SAM's window keeps the system's language, even when the window itself shows another one. | ✅ |
| [`only_a_folder_with_a_compiled_catalogue_holds_translations`](../crates/sam-core/src/i18n.rs#L248) | A folder counts as holding SAM's translations only with a compiled catalogue for at least one language: an empty folder, or one with language folders but no `sam.mo` (another program's), is passed over for the next place to look. | 🆕 new |

| [`every_text_the_window_builds_is_translated`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/i18n.rs#L158) | Every text the window shows goes through the translations (audit part 5: "1 app selected", "{n} apps selected" and the Permission Manager's switch names stayed English in every language). | 🆕 new |

## I. Foundations

<a id="38"></a>
### 38. SAM's own files survive: files from older versions are migrated, files from newer versions stay untouched, and damaged files are moved aside without stopping SAM.

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`a_complete_configuration_file_loads_every_setting`](../crates/sam-core/src/config.rs#L503) | A complete configuration file with every section loads without loss. | ✅ |
| [`settings_change_by_key_and_old_theme_names_are_migrated`](../crates/sam-core/src/config.rs#L637) | Settings can be changed by key, and old theme names are migrated to Light, Dark or System. | ✅ |
| [`settings_read_back_by_key_and_text_is_taken_literally`](../crates/sam-core/src/config.rs#L664) | `sam-jail config get` reads back what is set; an unknown key is an error. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_settings_file_that_cannot_be_read_is_an_error_and_stays`](../crates/sam-core/src/config.rs#L695) | A settings file that exists but cannot be read (not text, or a folder) is an error that names it, and it stays as it is: the stricter settings in it are never silently replaced by the defaults, which a later save would write over it. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_current_settings_file_is_not_migrated_again`](../crates/sam-core/src/config.rs#L722) | A file already in the current format is not migrated again: an old theme name in it is an invalid value and is reset to System. | 🆕 new |
| [`files_from_a_newer_sam_are_never_overwritten`](../crates/sam-core/src/format.rs#L102) | SAM refuses to overwrite a file written by a newer SAM and reads files without a format number as format 0. | ✅ |
| [`access_from_bool_and_str`](../crates/sam-core/src/permissions.rs#L1463) | Folder access is read from both the old form (true/false) and the current form ("ro", "rw"). | ✅ |
| [`old_profiles_with_removed_options_still_load`](../crates/sam-core/src/permissions.rs#L1744) | Settings saved with options SAM no longer has still load, with the removed options mapped to their safe meaning. | ✅ |
| [`permissions_saved_as_toml_load_back_unchanged`](../crates/sam-core/src/permissions.rs#L1797) | Permissions saved as TOML load back unchanged. | ✅ |
| [`old_extra_backend_args_still_load`](../crates/sam-core/src/permissions.rs#L1810) | Raw sandbox arguments saved under their old name still load. | ✅ |
| [`presets_in_the_older_flat_format_still_load`](../crates/sam-storage/src/lib.rs#L1366) | Presets in the older flat format still load. | ✅ |
| [`old_formats_are_migrated_and_kept`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/robustness.rs#L65) | Files from older SAM versions are migrated to the current format, and the originals are kept. | ✅ |
| [`newer_formats_are_left_untouched`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/robustness.rs#L106) | Files from a newer SAM are never changed by an older one. | ✅ |
| [`damaged_files_are_moved_aside_and_reported`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/robustness.rs#L151) | Damaged files are moved aside and reported, and SAM carries on. | ✅ |
| [`mangled_files_never_crash`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/robustness.rs#L202) | Mangled files never make SAM crash. | ✅ |
| [`empty_or_relative_folder_settings_are_ignored`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/odd_names.rs#L217) | An empty or relative folder setting is ignored (as the XDG rules say) instead of putting SAM's files into whatever folder SAM was started in (audit G-F20). | ✅ |
| [`one_unreadable_setting_does_not_cost_the_others`](../crates/sam-core/src/config.rs#L586) ⟲ | Regression (audit U-L4): one value of the wrong kind (`max_files = "x"`) or an unknown word made the whole file unreadable, and SAM used the defaults for everything, losing stricter choices such as "never offer compatibility mode". | 🆕 new |
| [`data_of_an_unreadable_record_is_not_offered_for_deletion`](../crates/sam-runner/src/ops/maintain.rs#L2330) ⟲ | Regression (audit U-L4): the data of an app whose record SAM could not read (damaged, or written by a newer SAM) was listed as left over, and "remove leftover data" deleted the app's private home. | 🆕 new |

**Code:** [`sam-core/src/format.rs`](../crates/sam-core/src/format.rs), [`sam-core/src/config.rs`](../crates/sam-core/src/config.rs), [`sam-core/src/permissions.rs`](../crates/sam-core/src/permissions.rs), [`sam-storage/src/lib.rs`](../crates/sam-storage/src/lib.rs)

**Also tried by:** [`tests/upgrade/run.sh`](../tests/upgrade/run.sh)

<a id="39"></a>
### 39. SAM's parts stay cleanly separated: the window reaches the core only through the command line and decides by codes, never by reading messages, and the parts that handle untrusted content never depend on the higher ones.

*An internal guarantee: it keeps SAM maintainable and is not a feature users see.*

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`times_are_shown_without_a_date_library`](../crates/sam-core/src/util.rs#L1021) | Times are shown without a date library: UTC dates exactly, local times in the computer's own zone and shape (chrono was dropped when it became unmaintained, RUSTSEC-2026-0543). | ✅ |
| [`the_permission_model_depends_on_no_other_part`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/architecture.rs#L59) | The permission model is the base: it depends on no other SAM part, so every other part can use it without creating a cycle. | ✅ |
| [`the_window_reaches_sam_only_through_the_command_line`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/architecture.rs#L67) | The window contains no sandbox logic: it depends only on the permission model and reaches everything else through `sam-jail --json`, so the window and the command line always behave the same. | ✅ |
| [`lower_parts_never_depend_on_higher_ones`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/architecture.rs#L75) | The parts that handle untrusted AppImage content or build the sandbox depend only on the parts below them, never on the operations layer, the command line or the window. | ✅ |
| [`the_sandbox_builder_depends_only_on_the_permission_model`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/architecture.rs#L98) | The sandbox command builder depends only on the permission model, so what it builds follows from the permissions alone. | ✅ |
| [`the_component_diagram_shows_the_real_dependencies`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/architecture.rs#L105) | The component diagram in docs/architecture.md shows exactly the real dependencies between the parts: no arrow is missing and none is invented. | ✅ |
| [`diagram_labels_are_quoted_so_github_draws_them`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/architecture.rs#L155) | Every box and arrow label in the documents' diagrams is quoted, so GitHub draws them: unquoted brackets or semicolons inside a label are a parse error there (the component diagram once failed that way). | ✅ |
| [`version_reports_the_json_interface`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/robustness.rs#L241) | `sam-jail version` answers with the JSON interface version even when the configuration cannot be read. | ✅ |
| [`an_error_code_survives_added_context`](../crates/sam-core/src/error.rs#L144) | An error code survives added context. | ✅ |
| [`a_required_run_does_not_skip`](../crates/sam-runner/src/contracts.rs#L906) | Where a run must prove everything (CI), a skip is an error; locally it is a note. | ✅ |
| [`skips_are_refused_in_ci_and_always_written_down`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/skip_reporting.rs#L35) | Skips are refused in ci and always written down. | ✅ |
| [`a_failing_section_is_named_and_the_next_still_runs`](../crates/sam-runner/src/contracts.rs#L885) | A failing section is named and the next still runs. | ✅ |
| [`a_skipped_test_is_recorded_with_its_reason`](../crates/sam-runner/src/contracts.rs#L728) | A skipped test is written to the skip log with its name and reason, one line each, so a run can report it instead of passing it silently. | ✅ |
| [`tests_find_the_helper_of_their_own_build`](../crates/sam-runner/src/lib.rs#L129) | A test program inside Cargo's build folder finds the helper of its own build, so tests that need it run instead of skipping; an installed program never looks above its own folder. | ✅ |
| [`a_build_never_takes_a_helper_from_path`](../crates/sam-runner/src/lib.rs#L105) | A program of a Cargo build whose helper is not built gets none, even when one is on PATH (an installed SAM or another build): the test then says it cannot run, instead of checking a different version. | ✅ |
| [`rooted_paths_have_a_runtime_folder_of_their_own`](../crates/sam-core/src/paths.rs#L391) | Rooted paths (tests) keep their runtime folders apart from SAM's real one and from every other root, and short enough for SAM's sockets. | ✅ |
| [`a_test_inside_a_test_keeps_its_runtime_folder_out_of_tmp`](../crates/sam-core/src/paths.rs#L428) | A root inside a test whose runtime folder is already one of these gets its folder next to it, never in /tmp (16,304 were left there by one mutation run, 2026-11-20); a long runtime folder still falls back to /tmp. | ✅ |
| [`every_build_follows_the_standard_config_and_data_folders`](../crates/sam-core/src/paths.rs#L325) | The standard `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` and `XDG_DATA_HOME` move SAM's folders in every build, a release build too, also while SAM's own folder switches are set and ignored. | 🆕 new |
| [`empty_or_relative_folders_are_ignored_in_every_build`](../crates/sam-core/src/paths.rs#L343) | An empty or relative folder is ignored in every build, for SAM's switches and the XDG folders alike: "" or "data" would put SAM's files into whatever folder SAM was started in (audit G-F20). | 🆕 new |
| [`an_error_names_its_sam_function_and_keeps_its_message`](../crates/sam-core/src/error.rs#L120) | The code of an error survives being wrapped in context, so the window recognizes it whatever language the message is in. | ✅ |
| [`json_run_keeps_its_reply_alone_on_standard_output`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/odd_names.rs#L112) | With `--json`, SAM's standard output is its reply and nothing else: an app that prints a line looking like the reply cannot be read as SAM's. | ✅ |
| [`preset_names_are_never_read_as_options`](../crates/sam-gtk/src/backend.rs#L372) | Preset names reach SAM after `--`, so one that starts with `-` is not read as an option (audit G-F20). | ✅ |
| [`runtime_folders_go_into_the_sessions_runtime_folder`](../crates/sam-core/src/paths.rs#L454) | Runtime folders (sockets, the passwd file of each start) go into the session's runtime folder when there is one, never onto the disk or into the folder SAM was started in; only without one into SAM's data folder. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_first_run_guide_is_marked_done_and_reset`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/interface.rs#L341) | The window's first-run guide is shown until it is marked done, and again after a reset: the mark is kept in SAM's settings folder. | 🆕 new |
| [`each_command_names_its_sam_function`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/src/main.rs#L1692) | Each command's errors name the SAM function it carries out (docs/functions.md), and the commands outside SAM's functions name none (mutation run: every answer could be dropped or be any number). | 🆕 new |
| [`man_writes_a_page_per_visible_command_and_none_for_hidden_ones`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/man_pages.rs#L8) | Man writes a page per visible command and none for hidden ones. | 🆕 new |
| [`private_services_end_with_the_test_process_that_started_them`](../crates/sam-runner/src/testenv.rs#L274) | TEST-LEAK (2026-11-23): a test process killed while it holds private services takes them along, and they never held one of its descriptors: a pipe the test process inherited reaches its end once the test process is gone, and both services have ended. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_test_that_fails_partway_leaves_no_gpg_agent_running`](../crates/sam-runner/tests/gpg_agent_guard.rs#L22) | A test that panics while it holds the guard leaves no gpg-agent running and no socket folder behind. | 🆕 new |
| [`every_man_page_named_is_written`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/src/man.rs#L79) | Every page a man page names (`sam-jail-config-get(1)`) is written too (audit: `sam-jail-config.1` named six pages that did not exist). | 🆕 new |
