<a id="42"></a>
### 42. SAM is checked and released as it says: the release key signs only files the release build of that very tag made, source files that match the package recipe and checksum lists the signing script writes itself; a failed release build leaves no tag; the owner's approval counts only for the version the owner saw and is judged by the base branch's checker; the CI scripts fail when the tests could not run; the programs SAM ships contain none of its test and release tools, and every path that builds or uses a program SAM ships or signs (the AppImage build, which the release workflow runs through make appimage, the release and signing scripts and the package recipes' build steps, which builders cannot skip like their test steps, but not the Makefile's cli target, which a developer runs for local use) refuses a built program that follows SAM's developer variables, checked with an empty environment and every folder in a temporary folder; and every workflow keeps pinned actions, least rights and checked downloads.

*An internal guarantee: it keeps SAM maintainable and is not a feature users see.*

| Test | Promise | Result |
|---|---|---|
| [`the_release_key_signs_no_appimage_the_release_build_did_not_make`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1339) | The release key signs no AppImage that the release build did not make: a draft can be edited by anyone with access, and GitHub's build provenance is what tells a changed file apart (audit P1). | 🆕 new |
| [`an_appimage_built_for_an_older_release_is_not_signed`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1363) | An AppImage that the release build made for an older tag is refused: only provenance from this tag's run counts, so an older, perhaps vulnerable version cannot be signed as the new one (audit P1, a replay). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_file_added_to_a_draft_release_stops_the_signing`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1422) | A file someone added to the draft stops the signing: the release key signs only files that were checked or written here, so neither a new file nor a .sha256 without its AppImage gets SAM's signature (audit P1). | 🆕 new |
| [`only_the_signing_tool_signs_a_release`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1378) | Only the signing tool signs: a release build with SAM's release tools on (owner decision of 2026-11-27), so signing runs like the shipped program, with developer variables off. | 🆕 new |
| [`a_signing_tool_that_follows_developer_variables_signs_nothing`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1401) | A signing tool that follows SAM's developer variables, a developer build or a release build whose debug assertions Cargo settings from outside the repository turned on, signs nothing: the script asks the tool before it uses the release key. | 🆕 new |
| [`source_files_that_differ_from_the_package_recipe_are_not_signed`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1437) | Source tarballs that differ from the ones the maintainer built (their checksums in the PKGBUILD) are refused before anything is signed (audit P13). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_checked_draft_is_signed_with_its_checksum_lists_written_again`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1455) | A draft whose files all check out is signed, and the checksum lists the release key signs are written here from the checked files: a swapped SHA256SUMS or .sha256 in the draft never gets SAM's signature (audit P1). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_release_that_fails_to_build_leaves_no_tag`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1749) | A release whose build fails after the release commit leaves no tag behind: the tag starts the release workflow, so it is set only after everything was built (audit P14). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_release_script_runs_no_tests_on_its_own_computer`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1775) | The release script never starts the test suite on the computer it runs on (the tests start real sandboxes and SAM's programs): it runs the program SAM_RELEASE_TEST_COMMAND names, with the checkout's path and SAM_REQUIRE_SANDBOX=1, and stops before the release commit when that program fails; with neither that nor SKIP_TESTS=1 it stops before it changes anything and names both. | 🆕 new |
| [`release_builds_use_one_fixed_rust_and_stop_on_overflow`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L139) | Release builds use one fixed rust and stop on overflow. | 🆕 new |
| [`release_builds_never_turn_on_debug_assertions`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L336) | SAM's developer variables and test hooks (SAM_CLI, SAM_RUN, SAM_LOCALE_DIR, SAM_DBUS_LOG, SAM_TRUST_TOOLS_IN, and the folder variables SAM_CONFIG_DIR, SAM_DATA_DIR, SAM_APPLICATIONS_DIR and SAM_RUNTIME_DIR) work only where debug assertions are on (owner decision 16 of 2026-11-23). | 🆕 new |
| [`cargo_config_only_replaces_sources`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L529) | The package builds unpack the vendor tarball next to the source, and with it the Cargo configuration `scripts/make-release.sh` writes (`cargo vendor`'s answer): it only points Cargo at the vendored crates, so it passes. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_release_build_check_refuses_a_program_that_follows_developer_variables`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L714) | The release-build check asks the built program itself, because Cargo takes profile settings from outside the repository too. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_release_stand_in_answers_like_the_real_program`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L792) | The release stand-in's answer to `config path` is laid out as the real `sam-jail` lays out its own: the check reads the real program's lines, not only the stand-in's. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_release_build_check_keeps_the_program_inside_a_temporary_folder`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L613) | The release-build check cannot reach the real home folder or the real SAM folders even when the built program is wrong: the program starts in an empty environment, inside a temporary folder of the check's own, with HOME, every XDG folder, TMPDIR and SAM's folder switches pointing into it, and nothing of the caller's environment besides PATH. | 🆕 new |
| [`release_build_paths_check_the_programs_they_build`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L855) | Cargo also takes profile settings and compiler flags from outside the repository (the builder's environment, Cargo configuration files in CARGO_HOME or above the source), which no test of the source can read. | 🆕 new |
| [`release_test_builds_stop_at_the_test_guard`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L998) | A release build ignores SAM's developer variables (folders and tools), so a test built in release mode would reach the real SAM folders or real tools. | 🆕 new |
| [`package_test_steps_build_at_optimisation_level_zero`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1090) | Unless debug assertions are set explicitly, rustc turns them off at every optimisation level above 0, and the distributions' flags may set one (Fedora's set `-Copt-level=3`). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_owners_label_counts_only_right_after_it_is_set`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1594) | The owner's label counts only on the event of setting or removing a label, i.e. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_approval_check_runs_the_base_branchs_checker`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1641) | The approval check runs the checker, with its settings (keeper.toml), as the base branch has them, never the pull request's own copies: a change cannot rewrite the check that judges it (audit P2, the part a pull request's workflow run can keep). | 🆕 new |
| [`a_desktop_test_run_that_does_not_build_fails`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1858) | The sandbox tests in the desktop session are judged by cargo's exit status: a run that does not even build prints no "FAILED" and must still fail (audit D-M11). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_website_is_not_built_with_a_changed_diagram_script`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/automation.rs#L1898) | The website serves its diagram script only when it is exactly the one checked: a changed download stops the build before the site is made (audit P8). | 🆕 new |
| [`the_test_and_release_commands_are_left_out_of_release_builds`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/shipped_programs.rs#L79) | `sam-jail dev make-fixture`, `bench`, `set-update-info`, `make-zsync` and `sign-appimage` are defined only in dev_tools.rs, and that module, the benchmark and the command that reaches them are behind the switch. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_window_screenshot_options_are_left_out_of_release_builds`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/shipped_programs.rs#L136) | `--screenshot`, `--size`, `--delay` and `--focus-walk` are read only in screenshot.rs, and the window uses that module only behind the switch. | 🆕 new |
| [`the_test_helpers_of_the_libraries_are_left_out_of_release_builds`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/shipped_programs.rs#L160) | The test AppImages and the test signing are left out of release builds of the libraries too. | 🆕 new |
| [`release_builds_never_turn_the_tools_on`](../crates/sam-jail-cli/tests/shipped_programs.rs#L176) | Nothing turns the switch on for what SAM ships: the packages, the Makefile and the build of the AppImage's programs never ask for the dev-tools feature. | 🆕 new |

✅ passed in the newest version report · ❌ failed there · 🆕 new: added after that report, so it has no result yet (every test passes before it is committed; the next report fills it in) · ⟲ a regression test: it guards against a bug that really happened.
